---

> Cikakken bayani na kowane environment variable da OmniRoute ke ganewa.
> Don samfurin farawa cikin sauri, duba [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Dole ne kowane variable da aka rubuta a nan ya bayyana a cikin `.env.example`, kuma
> kowane variable da ke cikin `.env.example` dole ne ya bayyana a nan. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> yana tabbatar da wannan yayin commit da kuma a cikin CI. Don tsallake wani variable da gangan, ƙara shi zuwa
> allowlist da ke cikin `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Sirran da Ake Buƙata

Sirran da ba a saita ba waɗanda aka yi wa alamar **Eh** ana ƙirƙirar su ta atomatik a farkon aiki (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) sannan a adana su a `server.env` cikin kundin bayanai — saita su kai tsaye idan wannan kundin na wucin gadi ne. Banda wannan shi ne `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: ba a adana shi a `server.env` — wuraren shigar uwar garken suna ƙirƙirar ƙimar wucin gadi ta kowace kunnawa wadda gada da ke cikin tsari kawai za ta iya amfani da ita, saboda haka abokan cinikin gada na waje suna buƙatar a saita ta kai tsaye.

| Sauyi                        | Wajibi           | Tsoho                         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------- | ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Eh**           | _(babu)_                      | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Yana sanya hannu/tabbatar da duk kukis na zaman dashboard (JWT). Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Eh**           | _(ana ƙirƙira ta atomatik)_   | `src/shared/utils/apiKey.ts`                              | Sirri da ake amfani da shi wajen ƙirƙira da tabbatar da checksum na maɓallan API da aka ɗaure da na'ura. Idan ba a saita shi ba, tsarin farawa na yau da kullum yana samar da shi kuma yana adana shi a `server.env` idan ana iya rubuta wa fayil ɗin. Saita shi kai tsaye idan kundin bayanai na wucin gadi ne. Ƙirƙire shi da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | A'a              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Yana saita kalmar sirrin dashboard lokacin farawa muddin ba a riga an adana wata kalmar sirri ba; ana yin watsi da shi bayan an adana kalmar sirri. Shigarwar npm da ta lambar tushe suna kwafe `.env.example` zuwa `.env`, don haka suna farawa da sanannen ƙimar riƙon wuri `CHANGEME`: maye gurbinta kafin kunnawa ta farko, ko saita `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (fanko) a cikin wannan `.env` domin ƙirƙirar kalmar sirri a mayen fara amfani a ziyarar farko, kamar yadda ƙimar da ba a saita ba (tsohuwar ƙimar hoton Docker) take yi. Sauya kalmar sirri da aka adana a Dashboard → Settings → Security, ko gudanar da `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` a cikin kwafin lambar tushe) tare da saita `DATA_DIR` zuwa kundin bayanan uwar garken. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Eh** (samarwa) | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Sirrin da aka raba don gadar WebSocket ta Codex Responses ta ciki. Yana tantance buƙatun gada tsakanin mai isar da WS na Electron/mai lilo da OmniRoute. ⚠️ **ANA BUƘATARSA a samarwa — idan ba a saita shi ba, ana ƙin duk buƙatun gadar WS.** Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | A'a              | _(SHA na git)_                | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Takamaiman id na karya cache na service-worker don harsashin PWA (#11779); shi ne na farko a jerin tantancewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | A'a              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Na biyu a jerin — masu gina PaaS (misali irin na Heroku) ne ke saita shi a matsayin commit ɗin da aka tura.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | A'a              | _(an samo)_                   | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Ƙimar jama'a ta lokacin ginawa da abokin ciniki ke amfani da ita don yin rajistar `/sw.js?v=…`; ana samo ta daga biyun da ke sama, sannan SHA na git.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | A'a (atomatik)   | _(atomatik a kowace kunnawa)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Sirrin kowane tsari da ke tabbatar da cewa tambarin IP na amintaccen takwara ya fito daga uwar garken HTTP ta OmniRoute (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware na authz yana amincewa da kasancewar buƙata a gida (takaita hanyoyin LOCAL_ONLY zuwa loopback/LAN) ne kawai idan tambarin yana ɗauke da wannan token. Ana ƙirƙirarsa ta atomatik a kowace kunnawa — bar shi ba a saita ba; saita shi tabbatacce kawai don tsarin matakai da yawa waɗanda dole ne su raba tambarin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | A'a              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | Jerin IP ko kewayon CIDR na reverse proxies da aka raba da waƙafi, waɗanda jerin yarda/ƙin IP zai iya amincewa da `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` nasu, ban da loopback, adireshin hanyar sadarwa mai zaman kanta da gefunan Cloudflare, waɗanda ake amincewa da su koyaushe. Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai don proxy da ke kan wani adireshin jama'a dabam; in ba haka ba, adireshin proxy ɗin kansa ne matatar za ta tantance. Takaita yawan buƙatu na kowane abokin ciniki na relay (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) yana amfani da wannan adireshin abokin ciniki da aka tantance, ba kan taken turawa kaɗai ba.                                                                                                                                                         |

### Umarnin Ƙirƙirawa

```bash
# Ƙirƙiri dukkan sirran huɗu a lokaci guda:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Kada a taɓa commit na fayilolin `.env` masu sirri na gaske zuwa tsarin sarrafa sigogi. `.gitignore` ya riga ya ware `.env`, amma tabbatar kafin turawa.

---

## 2. Ma’ajiya & Rumbun Bayanai

OmniRoute yana amfani da **SQLite** (ta hanyar `better-sqlite3`) don duk adana bayanai na dindindin. Waɗannan sauye-sauye suna sarrafa wurin bayanai, ɓoyewa, da zagayowar rayuwarsu.

| Sauyi                                      | Tsoho                         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Babban kundin adireshi na SQLite DB, kwafin ajiyewa, da fayilolin bayanai. Sauya shi don kundin Docker ko hanyoyin da aka keɓance.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Hanyar kaucewa ga kariyar DATA_DIR ta gwaji/eval (#10428). Gwaje-gwaje da binciken eval/print na Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gami da siffofin `--eval=`/`--print=`) waɗanda ba su da `DATA_DIR` ana karkatar da su zuwa kundin temp na wucin gadi domin kada su buɗe ainihin rumbun bayanan mai gudanarwa; saita shi zuwa `1` don sake amincewa da amfani da ainihin kundin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA na artifact da ke gudana. Ana yi masa hatimi ta `npm run build:release`; ana iya shigar da shi cikin containers waɗanda ake rarrabawa ba tare da alamar `dist/BUILD_SHA` ba. Ana nuna shi a matsayin `system.buildSha` a kan `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Manunin da ƙofar tabbatar da asalin pack-artifact ke kwatanta build SHA da shi (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Saita shi zuwa `1` don ba da damar tattara build wanda SHA ɗinsa ba ya kan layin release, tare da yin rikodinsa a matsayin canary da aka yi da gangan maimakon hana shi wucewa ta ƙofar (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | Maɓallin API na gwajin smoke na tura canary, wanda ake aikawa a matsayin `Authorization: Bearer` zuwa `/v1/chat/completions`. Rubutun tura manhaja ne kawai yake amfani da shi (#10429), sabar ba ta taɓa amfani da shi. Ba shi da alaƙa da masu canjin `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` na tsarin gwajin smoke na CLI da ake kunna bisa zaɓi (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` a cikin `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — duba [Haɗin CLI → Ainihin zagayen gwajin smoke](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Alamar matakin ginawa (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` yana saita ta zuwa `1`, kuma duk ma’aikacin ginawa da aka ƙaddamar yana gadonta domin matakin DB ya mayar da stub marar aiki maimakon loda native addon na better-sqlite3 (wanda ke dakatar da ma’aikacin yayin fita). Ba a taɓa saita ta ga sabar da ke aiki ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Saita zuwa `1` domin tsallake binciken prebuild na native dependency na zaɓi don bishiyoyin vendored na musamman. Wannan ba ya sa dependency da ya ɓace ya zama abin ginawa; yi amfani da shi kawai idan ana samar da native dependencies ta wata hanya dabam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Madadin fallback** na `DATA_DIR`, wanda ake dubawa kawai idan ba a saita `DATA_DIR` ba. Ana amfani da shi don gano ma’ajiyar zaman thread-sticky ta PromptQL executor da ke kan faifai (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); idan ba a saita ko ɗaya daga cikin masu canjin ba, ma’ajiyar za ta kasance cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai (ba za ta dore bayan sake kunnawa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Kundin adireshin da **runtime plugin scanner** ke karantawa — kuma tushen da plugin manager ke girkawa a ciki — yana maye gurbin tsohon wurin da ake samo daga gida (#11827). Nuna shi zuwa bishiyar plugin da aka bind-mount a Docker/K8s maimakon matsar da HOME kawai don sauya wurin hanyar bincike (HOME kuma yana sarrafa duk sauran halayen da suka danganci kundin gida). Idan ba a saita ba = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ko `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` idan aikin bai fitar da wani gida kwata-kwata ba — wannan mai canjin yana kawar da rashin ganowa na shiru. Ana rubuta kundin adireshin da aka warware sau ɗaya yayin farawa a matsayin `scanner.dir_resolved` tare da shigarwar da ta yi nasara. Na ɓangaren saba kawai: plugins na umarnin CLI suna ci gaba da amfani da nasu `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` (sashe na 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(babu komai = a kashe)_      | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Maɓallin AES don cikakken ɓoye bayanan SQLite da ke ajiye. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Alamar sigar maɓallin ɓoyewa. Ƙara ta yayin juyawar maɓalli domin tallafa wa buɗe tsofaffin kwafin ajiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Idan `true`, yana tsallake ajiyar fayil ɗin SQLite na yau da kullum/kafin-rubutawa (ajiye/share farashin models.dev, rubutun saituna). Ajiyar hannu da ta kafin-maido da bayanai har yanzu suna gudana. Wannan **ba ya** kashe hoton kariya mai ɗorewa na tilas na mai gudanar da ƙaura ko kariyar ƙaura mai yawa don DB mai ɗorewa da yake akwai. Ana iyakance ajiyar da ba ta hannu ba zuwa sau ɗaya kawai cikin mintuna 60. **Settings → Storage** na Dashboard na iya kashe ajiyar atomatik ta yau da kullum daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_SLOW_MS`                 | _(ba a saita ba/a kashe)_     | `src/lib/db/adapters/slowDiagnostics.ts`              | Milliseƙan; ƙimomin da suka gaza 1 ko waɗanda ba lambobi ba suna kasancewa a kashe; yana yi gargaɗi aƙalla sau ɗaya kawai cikin kowane sakan 10; layin log yana da driver, operation, da elapsedMs kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_OPTIMIZE_ENABLED`        | `true`                        | `src/lib/db/sqlitePlannerStats.ts`                    | Kula da ƙididdigar planner a bango, dabam da tsaftacewa bisa lokacin riƙewa: zagayen tsaftacewa na farko da aka kunna (yawanci 30s bayan farawa, bayan migrations) yana gudanar da `PRAGMA optimize=0x10002`; zagayen da ke biyo baya kowane 6h suna gudanar da `PRAGMA optimize` (ko `PRAGMA optimize=0x00002` don bincike marar iyaka). `false` ko `0` yana kashe duk wannan kulawa (ba a bambanta manyan da ƙananan haruffa, kuma ana cire fararen sarari na gefuna). Ba ya shafar rumbunan bayanai a ƙwaƙwalwa ko hanyoyin ajiyar madadin masu karantawa kawai. Ana rubuta gazawa a log ba tare da jefa exceptions ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_ANALYSIS_LIMIT`          | `400`                         | `src/lib/db/sqlitePlannerStats.ts`                    | Yana saita `PRAGMA analysis_limit` kafin ingantawa. Ana takaita cikakkun lambobi na tsarin goma zuwa [0, 100000]; ƙimomi masu mummunan tsari ko fanko suna amfani da ƙimar asali 400. `0` yana neman cikakken bincike marar iyaka na teburan da ingantawa ta zaɓa; zagayen lokaci-lokaci yana goge bit na iyakar samfurin SQLite optimize don bin wannan zaɓi. Ba a gudanar da wani umarnin SQL na bincike mai zaman kansa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Tsohon laƙabi** na `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Ana karɓarsa a matsayin madadin idan babban variable ɗin ba ya nan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Tsohon laƙabi** (nau'in da aka encode da Base64) da ake karɓa a matsayin madadin. Ana decode ɗinsa ta atomatik kafin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Sauya tazarar healthcheck na SQLite na lokaci-lokaci (ms). Idan ba a saita ba, ana samo tsoffin ƙimomi daga `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(an cire)_                   | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **An cire.** Gudanar da `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` na lokaci-lokaci a tsarin da ke aiki na iya lalata shared wal-index mapping kuma ya durƙusar da tsarin da SIGBUS (#13973), don haka scheduler ɗin ba ya wanzu yanzu. Variable ɗin ba ya aiki: ƙima mai kyau tana rubuta gargaɗin daina amfani sau ɗaya, yayin da `0` ko rashin saita shi ba ya fitar da komai. Ana kula da WAL ta hanyar checkpoints na PASSIVE (a ƙasa), kuma shutdown checkpoint ne ke truncate ɗinsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (5m)                 | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Sauya tazarar `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` da ake yi akai-akai (ms). Yana rage pending WAL frames don checkpoints su kasance masu sauri kuma fayil ɗin WAL ya kasance cikin iyaka tsakanin truncations na shutdown. `0` yana kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Idan zagayen PASSIVE ya gano cewa fayil ɗin WAL ya wuce wannan girman, a gudanar da `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` domin WAL ya sake farawa ba tare da sake rubuta wal-index da aka taswira ba. An cire checkpoints na yanayin truncate da ake yi kai tsaye (duba layin `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` domin fita daga process bayan matsanancin matsin albarkatu ya ci gaba har tsawon `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, ta yadda mai kulawa (systemd `Restart=always`, manufar sake farawa ta Docker) zai dawo da process mai tsabta maimakon ci gaba da bayar da 503s har abada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (minti 2)            | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tsawon lokacin da matsanancin matsin lamba dole ne ya ci gaba kafin fitar sake farawa ta kai ta kunna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` domin yin watsi da `/proc/pressure/memory` (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) na dukkan host idan maƙwabci mai hayaniya ya jawo 503s alhali wannan process ɗin yana aiki lafiya (#13124). Kariyar cgroup da V8 heap za su ci gaba da aiki. Haka kuma PSI zai daina ƙidayuwa a matsayin hujjar takurawar kernel ga `cgroup_high`: jimillar cgroup da ta haura `memory.high` za ta zama mai tsanani ne kawai idan an sami ƙaruwa a `high`/`max` na `memory.events`. Idan babu `memory.events`, za a riƙe ainihin rabo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_        | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Takamaiman path (cikakke ko mai dangantaka da cwd) zuwa `sql-wasm.wasm` yayin amfani da fallback adapter na WASM na `sql.js`. Idan ba a saita shi ba, ana gano shi ta atomatik ta hanyar dependencies na package da yiwuwar layouts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Adadin kwanakin da checkpoints, fayilolin shigarwa/fitarwa/kuskure da aka ambata, da kuma row na aikin Batch API da ya kai matakin ƙarshe (an kammala/ya gaza/an soke/lokacinsa ya ƙare) za su kasance kafin aikin tsaftacewa na atomatik ya share su. Wannan zai fara aiki ne kawai bayan an kunna `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ya yi daidai da wa'adin riƙe sakamakon Batch API na OpenAI. Ba ya shafar route ɗin `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` da operator ya kunna, wanda bisa tsari yake ci gaba da aiki ba tare da wani sharadin shekaru ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Idan `true`, a bar aikin tsaftacewa na atomatik ya share ayyukan Batch API da suka kai matakin ƙarshe (da checkpoints ɗinsu) bayan sun wuce `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, sannan ya share abun cikin BLOB na fayilolin da aka loda bayan sun wuce nasu `expires_at`. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa: kowace installation da ake da ita za ta ci gaba da riƙe wannan bayanin kamar yadda take yi a baya har sai operator ya zaɓi shiga. Haka kuma feature flag ne da za a iya gyarawa daga dashboard — duba `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake duba lafiyar DB gaba ɗaya yayin farawa. Yana da amfani ga ayyuka masu ɗan gajeren lokaci da gwaje-gwajen haɗawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` don tilasta kunna madaukin duba lafiyar DB, ko da kuwa a al'ada za a tsallake shi (misali, ayyuka masu ɗan gajeren lokaci).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake dumama muhallin aiki na asali yayin `npm install`. Yana da amfani a shigarwar CI/marasa allon nuni inda aka riga aka gina sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_        | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Sauya kundin adireshin da mai gudanar da ƙaura yake bincikawa. Yana da amfani yayin jigilar ƙaura da aka haɗa cikin ginannun sigogi na musamman.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ƙarin kundin adireshi na ƙaura a matsayin shigarwar `namespace=dir` waɗanda alamar raba hanyar dandamali ta raba (misali `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). Ana yin rikodin fayilolin da aka samu a wurin a matsayin `<namespace>-<number>`, saboda haka rarrabawar da ke jigilar ƙaurarta ba za ta taɓa karo da guraben lambobi na asali ba. Shiga marar tsari, namespace mara inganci, ko kundin adireshi da babu shi yana haifar da kuskure yayin farawa maimakon a tsallake schema a ɓoye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Iyakar tsaro ta yawan ƙaurorin da ke jiran aiwatarwa (#3416). Ana dakatar da farawa idan yawan ƙaurorin da ke jiran aiwatarwa ya fi wannan a kan DB da yake akwai (yana kariya daga teburin bin diddigi da aka goge). Ƙara shi don dawo da tsohon madadin; saita zuwa `0` don kashe wannan dubawar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Kundin aiki na shingen sakin `check:install-upgrade`. Yana buƙatar kusan 12 GB (itatuwan shigarwa biyu na kusan ~3 GB tare da tarball), saboda haka bai kamata ya gudana a kan ƙaramin tmpfs ba — a kan runner mai masaukin kansa, `/tmp` tmpfs ne mai goyon bayan RAM na 12 GB, kuma shingen ya cika shi, lamarin da ya datse kunshin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(tsoho a cikin lamba)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Tazarar flush (ms) ga mai rubuta kashe kuɗi/farashi a rukuni. Ƙananan ƙimomi suna rage haɗa rubuce-rubuce wuri guda; manyan ƙimomi suna rage takaddama a DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(tsoho a cikin lamba)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar kashe kuɗi da za a ajiye a buffer kafin a tilasta flush. Ƙara shi a turawa masu babban QPS; rage shi idan iyakantaccen amfani da ƙwaƙwalwa ya fi muhimmanci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Saita zuwa `"true"` domin fitar da rajistan gyaran kurakurai na `[ProxyFetch]` a hanyar relay ta Vercel. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa don kauce wa fallasa alamun zaɓin hanya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_BINARY_PATH`         | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/sync.ts`        | Cikakkiyar hanyar binary na proxy core na cikin gida (fayil mai suna `sing-box` / `mihomo`) wanda ke duba saitin core da aka samar kafin ya maye gurbin fayil ɗin da aka karɓa. Idan ba a saita ba, yana rubuta `<path>.generated` kawai a gefen fayil ɗin da aka karɓa. Don muhallin host kawai: ba a taɓa adana shi a database ko karɓarsa ta API na gudanarwa ba, saboda ana aiwatar da binary ɗin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Yadda proxy core na cikin gida ke sake lodawa bayan maye gurbin saitin da aka tabbatar: jerin argument na JSON da ake gudanarwa ba tare da shell ba, ko kuma daidai `external` lokacin da wani mai sa ido na waje ke amfani da fayil ɗin. Ba a taɓa adana shi a database ko fallasa shi ta API ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` domin haɗa IP na abokin ciniki/egress da prefix na account a layin rajistan process mai cikakken bayani na `[ProxyEgress]`. Ana barinsa a KASHE ta tsohuwa don kada rajistan process ya fallasa IP ko prefix na account.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Saita zuwa `1` domin buga bayanan binciken lokacin kowace buƙata (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) daga umarnin quota na CLI zuwa stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(ta atomatik)_               | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Takamaiman hanyar da binciken lafiyar kwantena ke gwadawa. Idan ba a saita ta ba, binciken yana samo ta daga `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; saita ta yana sake kunna amfani da ƙarshen sa ido mai zurfi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(ba a saita ba)_             | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa kowace ƙima marar fanko don fitar da bayanan bincike na `[omniroute completion]` daga hanyoyin ma'ajiyar kammalawar shell na CLI (karantawa/sabuntawa/rubutawa). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — waɗannan ma'ajiyoyin suna gaza ba tare da sanarwa ba domin ma'aji da ya ɓace ko ya lalace kada ya taɓa hana kammalawa da tab aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (awa 24)           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin lokacin sake gwadawa ga kowane abu na batch (ms). Abubuwan da suka wuce wannan lokaci ana yi musu alamar sun gaza.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Jinkirin tushe (ms) don exponential backoff yayin sake gwada abubuwan batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (awa 1)             | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Iyakar (ms) don exponential backoff tsakanin sake gwada abubuwan batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin adadin batch da ake sarrafawa a lokaci guda. Ƙara shi don ƙara yawan aikin da ake kammalawa; bar shi ƙasa don kauce wa cunkoson iyakance-ƙima.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Kafin canza rumbun bayanai mai ɗorewa da ke akwai, mai gudanar da ƙaura yana wallafa cikakken,
> snapshot mai adireshin-abun-ciki a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Wallafawa tana buƙatar tsarin fayil
> wanda ke goyon bayan hard links na tsarin fayil ɗaya waɗanda ba sa rubuta a kan abin da ke akwai, tare da daidaita fayil mai ɗorewa. Rundunan POSIX kuma
> suna buƙatar daidaita directory; a Windows, Node na iya ƙin karɓar handles na directory, saboda haka OmniRoute yana flush na
> fayil ɗin da aka wallafa kuma yana ɗaukar daidaita shigarwar directory a matsayin ƙoƙari gwargwadon iko.
> Idan `DATA_DIR` da aka mount ba zai iya samar da waɗannan tabbacin ba, farawa zai gaza cikin aminci kafin aiwatar da
> ƙaura. Matsar da `DATA_DIR` zuwa volume mai waɗannan primitives; kada a yi amfani da
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` don tsallake amincin ƙaura.

### Yanayi

| Yanayi                         | Saitawa                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ci gaba a kan na'ura**       | Bar duk tsoffin saituna yadda suke. DB yana a `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.              |
| **Docker**                     | `DATA_DIR=/data` + ɗora volume a `/data`.                                               |
| **An ɓoye bayanai a ma'ajiya** | Saita `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + adana kwafin ajiyar maɓallin! Rasa shi = rasa bayanai. |
| **CI/Gwaji**                   | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — na ɗan lokaci ne, ba a buƙatar ɓoyewa.                 |

---

## 3. Hanyar Sadarwa & Tashoshin Sadarwa

| Sauyi                                       | Tsoho                                                        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                                      | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Babban tashar sadarwa don Dashboard UI da maƙurar API duka (yanayin tashar sadarwa guda ɗaya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(babu komai = tushe)_                                       | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Ƙaramar hanyar URL don samar da OmniRoute a bayan reverse proxy (yana saita `basePath` na Next.js; turawar tabbatar da izini tana la'akari da basePath). Misali `/omniroute`. A cikin Docker, ana gina ƙimar a lokacin `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); hotunan tushe da aka riga aka gina za su iya amfani da wata ƙimar lokacin aiki sau ɗaya a farkon container kafin Next.js ya fara aiki. Saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa asalin adireshin jama'a wanda ya haɗa da wannan ƙaramar hanyar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(babu komai = tushe)_                                       | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Kwafin `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` da burauza ke iya gani, wanda ake saka shi kai tsaye a lokacin gini domin nunin maƙurar dashboard ya nuna `https://host/omniroute/v1` maimakon `https://host/v1`. Yana koma amfani da `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` idan ba a saita shi ba. Sake ginawa bayan canzawa (`basePath` na Next ana ƙayyade shi ne a lokacin gini).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(ba a saita ba = ba za a taɓa iya saka shi cikin frame ba)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Zaɓin kunna saka shafukan HTML cikin iframe. Idan ba a saita ba, kowace hanya tana zuwa da `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Saita zuwa `vscode` don gabatar da shafukan (dashboard, shiga, takardu, shafin sauka) tare da `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` kuma ba tare da `X-Frame-Options` ba, domin VS Code Simple Browser ya iya nuna su (yanayin `dashboardOpen: "editor"` na OmniCopilot). Fuskar API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, laƙabban matakin tushe) tana ci gaba da amfani da tsauraran headers a kowane hali. `vscode` kaɗai ake ganewa — `1`/`true` ba sa kunna shi. Lokacin ginawa: sake ginawa bayan canza shi (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` don images; saita shi a kan shigarwar da aka riga aka gina ba shi da tasiri).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita, yana gabatar da proxy API na `/v1/*` (da Gemini-native `/v1beta/*`) a wannan keɓantaccen port.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                                    | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adireshin bind na port ɗin API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita, yana gabatar da Dashboard UI a wannan keɓantaccen port.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(an kashe)_                                             | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Yana iyakance haɗin chat masu gudana a lokaci guda; buƙatun da suka zarce iyakar suna samun `503` tare da `Retry-After`. Cikakkiyar lamba mai kyau tana kunna kariyar; rashin saita ta ko `0` yana kashe ta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Tsayayyen ID na musamman na wannan gateway yayin haɗa matsalolin OmniRoute a jere. Yana ba da damar binciken madauwari tsakanin takwarori masu shigowa. Haruffan da aka yarda da su: haruffa, lambobi, `.`, `_`, `:`, da `-`; mafi yawa haruffa 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | Tushen URLs na OmniRoute da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za su iya karɓar `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. URLs na upstream da aka amince da su a sarari kaɗai ne ke karɓar metadata na takwara; duk sauran masu samarwa ba a taɓa su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Matsalolin da aka maimaita ko ƙarewar adadin da aka ware suna mayar da HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
