| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                                  | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adireshin ɗaurewa na sabar WebSocket kai tsaye. Saita shi zuwa `0.0.0.0` don buɗe shi ga LAN (sannan kuma saita `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`). Haɗe-haɗe suna tabbatar da izini ta amfani da kukin zaman dashboard ko maɓallin API na Bearer wanda ke da ikon gudanarwa (`manage`/`admin`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Ƙarin tushen adireshi da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka yarda su buɗe WebSocket kai tsaye. An riga an ba da izinin tushen dashboard na loopback ta tsohuwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Ƙarin sunayen runduna da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka yarda da su don tushen WebSocket kai tsaye. Ba kamar `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (cikakkun URLs na tushe) ba, wannan yana daidaita ɓangaren runduna kawai — yana da amfani ga saitunan LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

A cikin ƙwaƙwalwa, ga kowane instance. `0` ko ƙimar da ba ta kai sifili ba tana kashe shingen ɓangaren IP (iyakar DB ta kowane token har yanzu tana aiki).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                                                 | Jigon Next.js                                                            | Yana sarrafa yawan bayanan logging, caching, bayyana cikakkun bayanan kurakurai, da ingantawar Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_ (zaɓin build ta atomatik)                  | `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs`                                  | Ga `npm run build`, ƙimar da ba a saita ba ko mai fanko tana zaɓar webpack idan ainihin iyakar ƙwaƙwalwa (RAM na zahiri ko ƙaramin rabon cgroup/process) ba ta wuce **16 GiB** ba ko ba za a tantance ta cikin aminci ba; sama da 16 GiB, ana zaɓar Turbopack. `0` da aka saita a fili yana tilasta webpack; `1` da aka saita a fili da sauran ƙimomi marasa fanko waɗanda ba sifili ba suna tilasta Turbopack. Ƙimar muhalli mai fanko da Compose ya tura **ba ta** tilasta Turbopack. Wannan mataki ya samo asali daga koma-bayan amfani da ƙwaƙwalwar build na 15.6 GiB (#18184); ba a yi benchmark na builds a host masu 24/32 GiB ba. Webpack na production yana tsallake cache ɗinsa na dindindin kai tsaye bisa asali (duba `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE` a ƙasa; #18141), kuma yana yin build da kololuwar RSS mai ƙasa sosai fiye da hanyar Turbopack ta yanzu. Development yana riƙe halayyar bundler da ke akwai. **Docker builds sun riga suna amfani da `0` (webpack) bisa asali**; yi amfani da `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` don kunna Turbopack a fili. Duba `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `next.config.mjs`                                                        | Gine-ginen production na webpack (`OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0`) suna tsallake persistent filesystem cache na webpack sai idan wannan yana da ƙimar `1`. Rubuta wannan cache yana ƙara kusan 2.5 GB ga kololuwar ƙwaƙwalwar gini a iyakar heap ta 6144 MB, kuma clean builds ba sa sake karanta shi. A saita zuwa `1` don adana `.build/next/cache/webpack` domin saurin maimaita gine-gine a kan na'ura mai isasshen RAM; gine-ginen Docker suna karɓarsa a matsayin `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE=1` (matakan builder na `Dockerfile` da `Dockerfile.bun`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake duba lafiyar amincin SQLite yayin farawa. Yana da amfani don saurin farawa a manyan ma'ajin bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | systemd (ka'idar sd_notify)                                              | systemd ne ke saita shi lokacin da aikin ke gudana ƙarƙashin service unit mai haɗin sd_notify; OmniRoute yana karanta shi (duba `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) don aika sanarwar READY/WATCHDOG. Mai sanarwar ba ya yin komai a wajen systemd a kowane hali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Mafi ƙaranci: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                                                     | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) na matsayin lafiyar bayanan shiga da aka adana a ma'ajiyar wucin gadi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                                                      | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` ko `true` don kashe gwajin haɗin masu samarwa na lokaci-lokaci a bango. A koyaushe ana cire masu samar da bincike (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS a cikin `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, misali `tavily-search`) daga zagayen binciken — "tantancewar" su ainihin tambaya ce zuwa sabis na waje da ake caji, don haka ba a taɓa duba lafiyarsu bisa jadawalin lokaci ba (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                                                          | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Saita zuwa `1` don bai wa mai kira da aka tantance damar buƙatar `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, wanda ke ɗaukar samfurin ɓangaren kammalawa sau ɗaya a kowane TTL. Masu kira marasa tantancewa ba sa taɓa kunna gwajin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                                                       | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Matsayin jinkirin tsayawa (ms) na mai rikodin event-loop na farawa: daskarewa da ta fi wannan tsawo tana rubuta layin `event loop stalled` guda ɗaya lokacin ci gaba, tare da ƙididdigar ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya da ayyukan da ke gudana, sannan tana ƙara ma’aunin/iyakar `eventLoopStall` a `/api/monitoring/health`. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa; ƙimar da ba za a iya karantawa ba ko marar kyau tana komawa zuwa `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                                                    | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Adireshin ɗaurewa na sabar dev/start ta Next.js. Yana maye gurbin tsohuwar ƙimar `0.0.0.0` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                                                  | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adireshin ɗaurewa da mai gudanar da Playwright ke amfani da shi lokacin ƙaddamar da Next.js. Tsohuwar ƙimarsa ita ce `127.0.0.1` don gwaje-gwaje masu keɓantaccen muhalli. **Kada a yi amfani da shi don `omniroute serve`** — a maimakon haka, yi amfani da `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` (POSIX shells suna saita `HOSTNAME` kai tsaye zuwa sunan na’urar; `.env` ba zai iya maye gurbinsa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                                                    | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Adireshin ɗaurewa na `omniroute serve`. Yana hana cin karo da canjin POSIX shell na `HOSTNAME` (wanda bash/zsh ke saita koyaushe zuwa sunan na’urar). Idan ba a saita shi ba, zai koma `0.0.0.0`. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

### Yanayin Tashoshin Sadarwa

```
┌──────────────────────── Tasha Guda (tsoho) ────────────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌────────────────────────── Rarrababbun Tashoshi ─────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                     │
│  Amfani: Bayyana API ga LAN yayin taƙaita Dashboard zuwa localhost.         │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌────────────────────────── Docker na Samarwa ───────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Yana daidaita tashoshin container da tashoshin host a docker-compose.prod.yml. │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Tsaro & Tantancewa

| Canji                                       | Tsoho                                                                                                      | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                                      | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Gishiri da ake haɗawa da masu gano kayan aiki don tantance sawun na’ura. Canja shi ga kowane turawa domin ware su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(ba a saita ba = gishiri na bazuwar da ake adanawa ga kowane girkawa a `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | Gishirin HMAC don samar da alamar tantancewar CLI ta gida. Saita wannan ƙima yana sabunta dukkan alamun CLI da ke kan na’urar, kuma koyaushe yana da fifiko kan gishirin da aka adana. Duba `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                                    | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Yana saita tutar `Secure` a kan kukis ɗin zaman. **Dole ne ya zama `true`** idan ana aiki a bayan HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                                    | API middleware                                           | Lokacin da aka kunna, abokin ciniki API proxy yana buƙatar takaddun shaida don `/api/v1/*` da `/v1/*`, gami da `GET /v1/models` a gaban mai sarrafa sa. Takaddun shaida suna bin manufofin API na abokin ciniki (maɓallan API ko ingantaccen zama). Katalogin kuma yana da rajistan mai sarrafa `requireAuthForModels`, wanda ba zai iya ƙetare wakili ba. Tare da kashe tutar duniya, kundin yana iya komawa `401`; cewa mayar da martani kadai ba ya tabbatar da inference an kare. Duba `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695). Ya yarda da `true`, `1` ko `yes`, yin watsi da harka da wuraren da ke kewaye; duk wata kima (ciki har da `on`) a kashe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                                    | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Yana ba da damar nuna cikakkun ƙimomin maɓallan API a cikin mahallin Dashboard. Ana iya daidaita shi daga Tutocin Fasalolin Dashboard; haɗarin tsaro ne a kan rabeɗɗun instans.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(empty)_                                                                                                  | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | ID ɗin maɓallan API da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda ke ƙetare rajistar buƙatu (don bin GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(unset = unlimited)_                                                                                      | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Kasafin buƙatun kowace rana na madadin da ake amfani da shi ga makullin API waɗanda ginshiƙin `rate_limits` ɗinsu yake null. Idan ba a saita shi ba ko babu komai: babu iyaka ta atomatik (#2289, #11017). `0` ma yana nufin haka (babu iyaka). Ingantaccen lamba N tana kunna N/rana, 5N/mako, 20N/wata. Ƙimomin da ba su da inganci kuma ba komai ba suna komawa ga tsohon tagogin 1000/rana, 5000/mako, 20000/wata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                                         | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Matsakaicin girman jikin buƙatar da aka yarda da shi. Yana ƙin payloads da suka wuce wannan iyaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                                          | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Jikunan buƙatu na ainihi waɗanda suke a ko sama da wannan matakin suna ɗaukar lasisin karɓar manyan buƙatu na atomatik, na cikin tsarin aiki, kafin tantancewar JSON (hanyar BYTE, har da `POST /v1/responses`). Ana amfani da irin wannan mafita ta [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) don barin isasshen sarari kamar yadda ake yi wa buƙatun da suka fi nauyi ta fuskar tsari; duk da haka har yanzu `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) ne ke iyakance shi, don kada [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ya dawo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                                         | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ana tilasta iyakar ƙarshe ta jikin hanyar chat bisa ga adadin bytes da aka karanta yayin shigarwa mai iyaka, har da buƙatun da `Content-Length` ɗinsu ya ɓace, bai inganta ba, ko ba ya faɗin gaskiya; abin da ya wuce iyakar yana karɓar `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(unset — no request-count cap)_                                                                           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** wannan tsohon iyakar adadin buƙatu yanzu yana aiki ne kawai idan an saita shi a sarari. Idan ba a saita shi ba (tsoho), ana tsara karɓar manyan buƙatun hira maimakon haka ta `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — kasafin BYTE da ake ƙayyade kansa bisa ainihin iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar ajiyar aikin a cikin **tsari guda** (tudun V8 guda). Buƙatun `/v1/responses` guda biyu masu kusan alamomin 750k da suka zo a lokaci guda na iya katse kusan Gi 12 na heap (#7849) — wannan gargaɗi ne na **kasafin ƙwaƙwalwa**, ba matsakaicin samfur mai tsauri na 2 ba. Tsari mai lafiya (heap ɗinsa yana ƙasa da rabon zubarwa) na iya karɓar ƙarin `/v1/responses` masu tsawo da ke gudana a lokaci guda ta `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Abokan cinikin SSE masu tsawo da dama (40–50) suna da alaƙa da heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ba “max 2” ba. Ƙara wannan a makance zuwa “amfani da host” zai sake jawo #7849. A ninka **heaps** da **N `DATA_DIR`s masu zaman kansu** (#11024); kada a taɓa amfani da `replicas>1` a kan fayil ɗin SQLite guda.                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(auto-derived)_                                                                                           | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** wannan shi ne canjin kasafin bytes na shigarwa da ake ƙayyade kansa (25% na mafi ƙanƙantar iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar V8/cgroup da aka raba da ninkawar wucin-gadi 8x). Ana takaita ƙimomin da aka ƙayyade kansu da waɗanda aka saita a sarari zuwa 8 MiB–2 GiB. Idan jiki ya fi kasafin da ake amfani da shi girma, zai gaza nan take da `413 body_exceeds_budget`; yayin da takaddamar albarkatu tsakanin jikkuna da kowannensu zai iya aiki ta kasance mai iya sake gwadawa tare da `503`. Abokan cinikin SSE masu tsawo 40–50 da ke gudana a lokaci guda suna dogara da wannan kasafin + heap, ba “max 2” mai tsauri ba. Karanta `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` a `/api/monitoring/health` kafin daidaitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Rabon zubarwa saboda matsin heap (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) don karɓar manyan buƙatun BYTE da STRUCTURE (#10183, #10268, #10437). Buƙatar manyan nauyi mai gudana a lokaci guda da ta wuce `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` za a zubar da ita ne kawai da `503` mai iya sake gwadawa idan heap ɗin SHIMA ya kai ko ya wuce wannan rabo; idan heap ɗin yana lafiya, ana karɓarta ta hanyar madaidaicin sararin heap mai lafiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (default `1`)                                                         | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙarin iyakar damar da aka takaita don hanyar sauri ta heap mai lafiya (#10437) a kan **STRUCTURE** da **BYTE** duka (`admitChatRequest`, har da jikkuna ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Ba tare da wannan iyaka ba, kowace buƙata mai aiki amma heap ɗinta lafiya tana ketare tsarin karɓa ba tare da wani rufi ba. Da zarar wannan adadin hayar aiki mai gudana a lokaci guda ya kasance ta hanyar heap mai lafiya, ƙarin buƙatun aiki za su koma hanyar jira/zirga iri ɗaya da aka iyakance wadda ake amfani da ita ƙarƙashin ainihin matsin heap. `0` yana kashe wannan ketarewar gaba ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_MESSAGE_COUNT`        | `200`                                                                                                      | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Yawan saƙonni da ke rarraba buƙatar tattaunawa a matsayin mai nauyi ko da jikinta bai kai iyakar bytes ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_TOOL_COUNT`           | `64`                                                                                                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Yawan kayan aiki da ke rarraba buƙatar tattaunawa a matsayin mai nauyi ko da jikinta bai kai iyakar bytes ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_ESTIMATED_TOKENS`     | `32000`                                                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙiyasin token na girman zare mai taka-tsantsan da ke rarraba buƙata a matsayin mai nauyi; wannan wakili ne na kuɗin karɓar buƙata, ba tsarin ƙididdigar token na biyan kuɗin mai samarwa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_MESSAGES`          | `0` (an kashe)                                                                                             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Iyakar tarihin tattaunawa ta zaɓi. A kashe take ta tsohuwa: adadin saƙonni manufar tura aiki ce, ba wata sifa ta duniya ta buƙata ba, kuma iyakancewa a nan yana ƙin tattaunawar da `413` na ƙarshe kafin bututun matsawa ya iya sa su zama masu aiki. `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` da tsarin rage matsin heap suna iyakance haɓakar heap. Saita ƙima mai kyau a tura ayyukan da ƙwaƙwalwarsu ta takaita kuma suke buƙatar iyaka mai tsauri; abin da ya wuce zai karɓi `413` mai tsari da ke buƙatar taƙaitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_NONSTREAMING_RESPONSE_BYTES` | `67108864` (64 MB)                                                                                         | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/nonStreamingResponseBody.ts` | Ƙaƙƙarfan iyakar girman amsar upstream da ba ta streaming ba wadda ake adanawa gaba ɗaya a ƙwaƙwalwa. Bayan wannan iyakar, ana soke mai karanta upstream kuma buƙatar ta gaza da wuri maimakon ci gaba da faɗaɗa string mara iyaka har sai heap ya cika.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARDING_HEADER_BUDGET_BYTES`  | `768`                                                                                                      | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/responseHeaders.ts`          | Matsakaicin adadin bytes na wire da ake turawa daga headers na amsar upstream. Idan an wuce wannan kasafin, ana cire headers masu ƙarancin fifiko (misali, na musamman `x-codex-*`, `x-oai-request-id`) don kasancewa cikin iyakokin headers na reverse-proxy da aka saba amfani da su. Saita shi zuwa mafi girma don tura ƙarin bayanan metadata daga upstream, tare da ƙarin girman headers na amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CORS_ORIGIN`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                                          | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Jerin izinin CORS na asali guda ɗaya na tsohon tsari. Don sabbin turawa, a fi son `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`. Ana amfani da CORS ne kawai ga abokan cinikin API na browser masu buƙatar cross-origin; rubuce-rubucen dashboard masu tantancewa suna amfani da buƙatun same-origin tare da kariyar CSRF mai alaƙa da zaman mai amfani maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                                          | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Jerin izinin CORS da aka raba da waƙafi. Ba a aika wildcard sai idan an saita `CORS_ALLOW_ALL=true` a bayyane.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CORS_ALLOW_ALL`                            | `false`                                                                                                    | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ƙofar tserewa ce da ake amfani da ita ne kawai a lokacin haɓakawa, don mayar da duk wani browser `Origin`. Kada a kunna ta a kan turawan rabawa ko na samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED`               | `true`                                                                                                     | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Wani tsohon suna ne. Ana karanta dashboard toggle ɗinsa (DB override) kafin wannan ƙimar muhalli; `false`, `0`, `no`, ko `off` a kowane ɗayansu yana kashe binciken host kamar yadda `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` yake yi, ko da an adana dashboard toggle na wannan flag a kashe; sauran ƙimomi suna barin guard ɗin ya dogara da flags biyu da ke ƙasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                                                    | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Yana kashe binciken host na outbound URL guard, har da toshe cloud-metadata (dukkan 169.254.0.0/16 da metadata.google.internal), a hanyoyin provider da ke bin yanayin guard: tantance URL, gano model, provider-node base URLs (har da remote rerank nodes), da gwajin proxy-fallback, da sauran hanyoyin outbound da ke da alaƙa da provider kamar remote image/media fetch. Haka kuma yana ba da damar private webhook targets, sai dai cloud-metadata/link-local endpoints, waɗanda suke ci gaba da kasancewa a kulle ko da an kunna wannan flag. Buƙatun chat da aka aika ta `BaseExecutor.execute()` suna ci gaba da yin binciken cloud-metadata. Masu samarwa na gida suna aiki da tsoffin ƙimomi: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (tsoho `true`) ya riga ya ba da damar local da LAN URLs a hanyoyin tantancewa, ganowa, da provider-node; gwajin proxy-fallback da private webhook targets suna duba wannan flag kaɗai kuma suna toshe local/LAN hosts yayin da yake a kashe. Yana rinjayar `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Ƙimar da aka adana daga dashboard toggle tana da fifiko kan wannan variable.                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                                     | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Yana ba da damar provider URLs a kan adireshin gida/na sirri (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, private ranges) don tantance provider, gano model, chat, da sauran hanyoyin outbound da ke da alaƙa da provider waɗanda yanayin guard ke sarrafawa. **Tsoho `true`** (local-first): daga nan guard ɗin yana toshe cloud-metadata endpoints — dukkan 169.254.0.0/16 da sanannun metadata hostnames (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Saita shi zuwa `false` don yanayin public-only, wanda yake toshe private da loopback hosts ma. Guard ɗin yana duba hostname ko IP literal yadda aka rubuta shi, kafin duk wani DNS lookup; a yanayin public-only, safe-fetch wrapper ɗin yana kuma warware hostname ɗin kuma ya ƙi duk wata amsa ta private. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` yana kashe waɗannan bincike a hanyoyin da layinsa ya lissafo. Masu samarwa na gida da aka gina a ciki, kamar LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, da Triton, suna tsallake duk binciken host don key validation da chat. Ƙimar da aka adana daga dashboard toggle tana da fifiko kan wannan variable. (#5066) |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                                                    | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Ba da damar hanyoyin `/v1/audio/*` (rubuce-rubuce, magana, fassarori) su yi amfani da kumburin mai samarwa mai jituwa da OpenAI da aka shirya a wajen localhost. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — tura sauti zuwa wani mai masaukin nesa yana canza asalin hanyar fita, kuma dole ne ya zama shawarar mai gudanarwa a sarari. Kumbunan loopback/masu zaman kansu (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ana ba su izini koyaushe kuma wannan bai shafe su ba. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                                                    | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Ba da damar `POST /v1/rerank` (da matakin rerank na loopback na injin ƙwaƙwalwa) ya yi amfani da kumburin mai samarwa mai jituwa da OpenAI da aka shirya a wajen localhost — kwamfuta a LAN ko abokin Tailscale da ke gudanar da TEI, Infinity, vLLM, da sauransu. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — tura zuwa wani mai masaukin nesa yana canza asalin hanyar fita, kuma dole ne ya zama shawarar mai gudanarwa a sarari. Kumbunan loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ana ba su izini koyaushe kuma wannan bai shafe su ba. Base URL na kumburin nesa dole ne kuma ya wuce manufar URL na fita ta mai samarwa (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(komai)_                                                                                                  | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Sunayen masu masauki (waɗanda aka raba da waƙafi ko sararin samaniya) waɗanda hanyar sadar da kumbin mai samarwa ke ɗauka a matsayin na gida — yawanci sunan sabis na Docker/Compose na reranker, TEI ko audio sidecar a cibiyar sadarwar Docker ta OmniRoute (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), don kada kumburin ya buƙaci tsayayyen IP na container. Sunayen da aka jera sun cancanci `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` da binciken lafiyar gida ba tare da kunna `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` ga kowane kumburin nesa ba, kuma ana kiran su kai tsaye — ba ta `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ko wakilin da aka kulle na haɗi ba. Har yanzu suna aika maɓallin API na haɗin. Ana karɓar sunaye masu lakabi guda ɗaya kawai da sunayen `.internal`/`.local`; cikakkun sunayen yanki na jama'a (FQDNs), lambobin IP da sunayen metadata na gajimare ana yin watsi da su tare da gargaɗi. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                                                    | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Lokacin da aka kunna OIDC, kashe shiga ta kalmar sirri domin masu amfani su iya tantance kansu ta OIDC Single Sign-On kawai. Ana kuma karɓar gajeren laƙabin `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`; tutar fasalin Dashboard mai maɓalli iri ɗaya ce ke da fifiko. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                                                    | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Laƙabi kai tsaye na `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_MFA`                     | `false`                                                                                                    | `src/server/auth/dashboardMfa.ts`                        | Farfaɗowar na’urar da ta ɓace don shiga dashboard mai tabbatarwa ta matakai biyu na TOTP da aka kunna da zaɓi. Idan `true` ne, ana tsallake mataki na biyu ne kawai yayin shiga daga host ɗin kansa (abokin haɗin loopback da `Host` na loopback); shigarwar daga nesa da `POST /api/cli/connect` daga wasu asali har yanzu suna buƙatar lambar. Shiga a cikin gida, kashe tabbatarwa ta matakai biyu a ƙarƙashin Saituna → Tsaro (ana iya barin lambar a sarari yayin da wannan yake a kunne, don a cire wani mataki da ba ya sake tabbatarwa; har yanzu ana buƙatar kalmar sirri), sannan a cire shi. TOTP yana rufe shiga da kalmar sirri da `omniroute connect` kawai — shigarwar OIDC/SSO tana dogara ne da mataki na biyu na mai samar da tantancewar kansa. Duba [AUTHZ_GUIDE](../architecture/AUTHZ_GUIDE.md#optional-totp-two-factor-login-16619).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Jerin Binciken Ƙarfafa Tsaro

```bash
# Mafi ƙarancin tsaro na samarwa (production):
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Yana buƙatar HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Tantance duk kiran proxy
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Kada a taɓa bayyana maɓallai a UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Iyakar 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Tsabtace Bayanai Masu Shigowa & Kare PII

OmniRoute yana samar da kariya mai matakai biyu: binciken yunƙurin kutse a ɓangaren buƙata da cire PII a ɓangaren martani.

> **⚠️ Iyakoki:** Waɗannan matakan kariya bincike ne na _hasashe gwargwadon ƙoƙari_, ba cikakken bangon kariya daga kutse ta hanyar prompt ko tsarin PII DLP ba. Za su iya haifar da sakamako na ƙarya (kamar yi wa prompts na persona/RPG marasa lahani alama) da kuma kasa gano barazana (kamar leetspeak, tazara, da tsarukan da ba na Turanci ba). Su kaɗai ba su isa wajen bin ƙa'idodi ba. Daidaita modes ɗin kuma gwada su da zirga-zirgarku kafin ku dogara da su.

### Ɓangaren Buƙata: Kariyar Kutse ta Prompt

| Maɓalli                           | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Kunna binciken saƙonni masu shigowa domin gano tsarukan kutse ta prompt.                                                                                                                           |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Manufofin kutse: `warn` = rubuta log kawai, `block` = ƙin buƙatar tare da 400. Tsohon `redact` **ba ya** cire rubutun kutse; yi amfani da `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` domin sake rubuta PII na buƙata. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Tsohon madadin suna na `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — halayya iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                                  |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`            | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Mafi ƙarancin matakin tsanani da `MODE=block` zai ƙi: `high` (tsoho), `medium`, ko `low`. Ana sa ido kawai kan tsarukan matsakaicin tsanani sai an rage matakin.                                   |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Tsohon madadin suna na `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — halayya iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                       |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Idan `true`, ɓoye PII a cikin buƙatu masu shigowa (ba tare da dogaro da yanayin kutse ba).                                                                                                         |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Ɓoye sanannun tsarukan API-key / secret-token daga bayanan buƙata/martani. Sai an zaɓa a kunna; yana kwaikwayon `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                                           |

### Ɓangaren Martani: Mai Tsabtace PII

| Maɓalli                          | Tsoho    | Fayil ɗin Tushe           | Bayani                                                                                 |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Bincika martanin LLM domin gano PII da ya fallasa kafin mayar da shi ga abokin ciniki. |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = ɓoye PII, `warn` = rubuta log kawai, `block` = watsar da dukkan martanin.   |

### Mai Tsabtace Context na Tokenized-Route na VS Code

| Maɓalli                             | Tsoho | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ----- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`   | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Yana cire context na active-editor da aka haɗa kai tsaye (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) daga buƙatun `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` kuma yana ɓoye abubuwan da ke cikin fayiloli masu muhimmanci da aka haɗa a bayyane. Amintacce ta tsoho; saita zuwa `0` domin kashewa. |

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayi                    | Saituna                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Bin ƙa'idodin kamfani** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (toshe kutse + ɓoye PII na buƙata/martani; modes ɗin ba su dogara da juna ba) |
| **Sa ido kawai**          | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — yana rubuta logs amma ba ya taɓa toshewa                                                                                                     |
| **Amfani na kashin kai**  | Bar duk a kashe — babu ƙarin nauyi                                                                                                                                                                         |
