## 6. Manufofin Kayan Aiki da Kewayawa

| Mai canzawa                                                 | Tsoho                        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                   | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                   | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                           | Yana sarrafa damar LLM na amfani da kayan aiki/kiran ayyuka. `allowlist` = kayan aikin da aka jera kawai, `denylist` = duk kayan aikin ban da waɗanda aka jera, `disabled` = babu takurawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json` | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`               | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin JSON mai ƙa’idojin sarrafa payload (gyare-gyaren upstream na kowane samfurin/protocol).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                       | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`               | Tazarar sake loda fayil ɗin ƙa’idojin payload (a millisecond) domin sabunta shi yayin aiki. Mafi ƙanƙanta ita ce `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                      | Zaɓi na son rai: yana tura ID ɗin samfurin `claude-*` marar prefix daga abokan cinikin Claude Code ta asusun Claude Code OAuth maimakon buƙatar prefix na mai samarwa. Bayyanannun prefix na mai samarwa har yanzu su ne ke da fifiko. Hakanan ana iya saita shi ta hanyar maballin kunnawa/kashewa a dashboard ɗin shafin mai samar da Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                       | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts`   | Saita shi zuwa `false` domin dakatar da OmniRoute daga ƙara beta na Anthropic `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` da kansa (tubalan tunani marasa sa hannu sai kawai). Buƙatun da ke da `thinking.display: "summarized"` suna tsallake shi ko da ya ya kasance; beta na redact da abokin ciniki ya yi shawararsa ko zaɓin `requestDefaults.redactThinking` mai dacewa da CC yana ci gaba da kunna shi. Buƙatun Claude OAuth suna riƙe beta ɗin (yana cikin tambarin Claude Code OAuth) sai dai idan an kunna tutar fasalin `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED`, wadda a tsoho take a kashe.                                                              |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                      | Halayyar tsohuwar `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` (#4825): tana maye gurbin duk wani sunan samfurin da ba shi da prefix da `defaultModel` na haɗin da aka zaɓa. A tsoho tana a kashe; `auto` ko samfurin da babu komai ne kawai ake maye gurbinsu, kuma tana rubuta gargaɗi guda ɗaya ga kowane ma’aurata idan an kunna ta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                          | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`                | Iyakar yawan buƙatun lokaci guda ga kowane samfurin a cikin combos na round-robin (#9100). An takaita semaphore na combo na round-robin zuwa buƙatu 3 masu gudana lokaci guda ga kowane samfurin, ba tare da wata damar sauya hakan ba, wanda ke jera zirga-zirgar da ke da babban concurrency a bayan wannan iyaka. An tabbatar da ƙimarsa ta zama `>= 1`, sannan an takaita ta zuwa `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENTIC_STALL_FAILOVER`                          | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/combo/agenticStallFailover.ts` | Zaɓi na son rai: saita zuwa `1` (ko `true`) domin idan wani memba na combo ya amsa wani juyi na sakamakon kayan aiki da `finish: stop` + bayani + sifili na kiran kayan aiki, a koma ga manufa ta gaba (dabarun madaukin fifiko, round-robin da zaman da aka kulle). Idan babu wata manufa da ta yi fiye, za a mayar da amsar da ta tsaya maimakon kuskure, kuma ba za a ɗora wa mai katsewar mai samarwa ko kulle samfurin wani caji ba. Ana riƙe juyawa masu yawo daga abokin ciniki har sai an kammala hukunci. Ba a rarraba buƙatun da ke amfani da siffar `input` ta Responses API ba. A kashe ta tsohuwa.                                             |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                      | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                   | Zaɓi mai haɗari na son rai da ke tsallake binciken OmniRoute na gida kan iyakar mahallin / iyakar alamomin shigarwa don buƙatun kai tsaye na samfurin guda. Masu samarwa na sama har yanzu suna tilasta ainihin iyakokinsu; matsa prompt da iyakar alamomin fitarwa ta samfurin da kansa suna nan aiki. Tsarin fifiko mai aiki shi ne: canjin Feature Flags DB > canjin muhalli > tsohuwa; ba a buƙatar sake farawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`            | Takardar YAML ta layi cikin `providers:` (RIC-738, D4). Idan an saita ta (tare da ko ba tare da tubalin `strategy:` ba), `/v1/chat/completions` yana karkata zuwa shigarwar OpenAI mai jituwa ta haɗin kai da ake karɓar baƙuncinta da kansa, maimakon tsarin cloud. Idan ba a saita ta ba (tsohuwa): hanyar tana wucewa kai tsaye zuwa tsarin cloud ɗin da yake akwai. Duba `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`            | Hanyar fayil ɗin YAML da ke ɗauke da takardar `providers:` iri ɗaya da ta `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, don tura tsarin da suka fi son fayil maimakon canjin muhalli na layi. Kowannensu yana kunna shigarwar da ake karɓar baƙuncinta da kanta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(unset — open route)_       | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`            | Maɓallin API na zaɓi da aka raba don shigarwar da ake karɓar baƙuncinta da kanta (tsarin D5, an tanada shi don tsarin ƙayyade adadin kowane maɓalli). Idan an saita shi, buƙatu dole su haɗa da `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Idan ba a saita shi ba: hanyar a buɗe take, daidai da tsarin mai samarwa na gida da ake karɓar baƙuncinsa da kansa da ke akwai (tura shi a loopback/cibiyar sadarwa amintacciya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`          | Takardar YAML ta layi cikin `strategy:` don injin karkatarwa mai ƙayyadaddun sakamako (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, mai katsewa mai lokacin sanyi, fifikon kuɗi, mai lura da jinkiri, sarkar madadin. Yana rinjayar tubalin `strategy:` na layi da aka saka cikin `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, bisa kowane maɓalli. Duba `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`          | Hanyar fayil ɗin YAML da ke ɗauke da takardar `strategy:` iri ɗaya da ta `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, don tura tsarin da suka fi son fayil maimakon canjin muhalli na layi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`        | Saita `1` domin dakatar da tattara tarihin tattaunawa. `resolveConversationId()` yana mayar da sakamakon da ba a bi diddigi ba kafin ya karanta SQLite ko ya fassara tarihin saƙonni, kuma hakan ya shafi ID na zaman da abokin ciniki ya bayar ma. Gudanar da zaman karkatarwa bai canza ba, kuma ba a goge bayanan da suke akwai ba. Don tsarin da ba ya amfani da kallon tattaunawa na dashboard kuma yake son a dakatar da ƙaruwa na teburin juyawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(ba a saita ba)_            | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`    | Maɓallin API don dabarar mai ba da hanya ta auto-combo ta `nadir` wadda aka zaɓa da gangan, ana amfani da shi lokacin da `config.nadir.apiKey` na combo ɗin kansa bai saita ba. Zaɓi ne: kira ba tare da maɓalli ba yana shiga matakin Nadir na anonymous, wanda ake iyakance adadinsa bisa IP, don haka ba ya aiki a bayan ƙofa ta raba-gateway. Mai muhimmanci; an fi so a yi amfani da filin na kowane combo lokacin da combo-oli ke amfani da asusu daban-daban.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`   | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`    | Tushen adireshin asalin API ɗin yanke shawara na Nadir, ana amfani da shi lokacin da `config.nadir.baseUrl` na combo ɗin kansa bai saita ba. Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai don Nadir da aka shirya a kan uwar garken kanka, wanda ke ajiye farkon prompt a cikin hanyar sadarwarka. Ana jure `/v1` a ƙarshen adireshin kuma a cire shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`              | Tagar mako-mako don iyakokin amfani na USD na kowane maɓallin API (tilasta buƙata, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: makon yana ƙarewa ne a farkon lokacin sake saiti na mako-mako daga cikin haɗin upstream da maɓallin zai iya isa gare su, ko kuma ya zama kwanaki 7 masu juyawa idan ba a san kowane lokaci ba. `calendar`: makon gida daga Litinin 00:00 zuwa Litinin ta gaba 00:00, iri ɗaya ga kowane maɓalli. Ana ba da shawarar `calendar` lokacin da rukunin asusun upstream guda ɗaya ake rabawa tsakanin maɓallan da aka ware wa kasafin kuɗi daban-daban. Ƙimomin da ba su inganta ba suna komawa zuwa `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(lokacin yankin process)_   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`              | Yankin lokaci na IANA (misali `Asia/Shanghai`) wanda ke bayyana Litinin 00:00 don tagar mako-mako ta `calendar`. Idan ba a saita shi ba ko kuma bai inganta ba: ana amfani da yankin lokacin aikin OmniRoute (wato `TZ` na host ko container, wanda sau da yawa ba a saita shi ba, wato Coordinated Universal Time, a cikin hotunan container). Ana yin watsi da shi a yanayin `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                        | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                    | Tsohuwar iyakar adadin tools da ake bari a cikin buƙata (tsohuwar OpenAI). Lambobi masu kyau kawai; idan ba a saita ƙimar ba ko kuma ba ta inganta ba, ana riƙe `128`. Mai bayarwa mai sanannen/tsayayyen iyaka (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) har yanzu yana amfani da nasa iyakar — wannan yana matsar da rufin tsoho ne kawai. Target-oli masu tsarin Claude ba su da tsohuwar iyaka (Messages API ba shi da ita); iyakar da aka koya har yanzu tana aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOOL_OUTPUT_IMAGE_MAX_COUNT`                     | `16`                         | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.ts` | Martani ga fassarar Taɗi: hotuna nawa na kayan aiki (`function_call_output`, `custom_tool_call_output`, rawar `tool`) aka sake aika azaman sassan hoton mai amfani akan buƙata. Ana adana sababbi kuma kowane babba ya zama maƙallin rubutu tare da `tool_call_id` sa. Lambobi masu inganci kawai; mara inganci ko mara inganci yana kiyaye `16`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOOL_OUTPUT_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES`                     | `20971520` (20 MB)           | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.ts` | Jimlar girman hoton kayan aiki-fitarwa URLs sake aika kowane buƙatu a cikin fassarar iri ɗaya. Hotunan da suka wuce kasafin kuɗi an fara jefar da su mafi tsufa, tare da gargaɗi ɗaya kowace buƙata. Lambobi masu inganci kawai; mara inganci ko mara inganci suna kiyaye `20971520`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(a kunne)_                  | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`                 | Kiran tools da aka kwaikwayi a prompt na SYNTX.ai: idan ba a saita shi ba ko kuma ya kasance `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes`, ana saka kundin tools na gida cikin prompt; kowace ƙima daban (misali `0`) tana tsallake wannan sakawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

## 7. URLs & Aiki Tare na Gajimare

| Sauyi                                       | Na Asali                                                  | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                  | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `BASE_URL`                                  | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL na gefen sabar da ayyukan aiki tare na ciki ke amfani da shi wajen kiran `/api/sync/cloud`. Bar wannan a matsayin URL na loopback/container ko da ana wakiltar manhajar ga jama'a ta hanyar proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLOUD_URL`                                 | _(babu komai)_                                            | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL na ƙarshen hanyar relay ta gajimare (fasalin premium).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `12000`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Iyakar lokacin HTTP don buƙatun aiki tare na gajimare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                   | `full`                                                    | Tsarin saitin ginin Webpack                      | Bayanan martabar lokacin gini (saita zuwa `minimal` don cire modules masu gata gaba ɗaya daga bundle).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                  | _fitarwar standalone ta .build/_                          | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`          | Sauyin lokacin gini don kundin adireshin fitarwa na standalone da matakin tattara wuri bayan gini ke amfani da shi. Ba saitin lokacin gudanarwa ba ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIST_DIR`                        | `dist/` (tushen repo)                                     | `scripts/build/prepublish.ts`                    | Sauyin lokacin gini don kundin adireshin da matakin wallafawa (`prepublish.ts`) ke rubuta bundle na CLI a ciki, maimakon `dist/` da ke ƙarƙashin tushen repo. Ana share kundin adireshin kafin ginin. Ba saitin lokacin gudanarwa ba ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`               | _(babu komai)_                                            | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Sirrin da aka raba wanda ake amfani da shi don tabbatar da sa hannun HMAC-SHA256 na martanin Cloud Sync.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`              | `false`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Saita zuwa `true` don bai wa ƙarshen Cloud Sync damar sake rubuta bayanan shaidar shiga na cikin gida. Tsoho shi ne `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE`    | `false`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Saita zuwa `true` don ƙin karɓar martanin Cloud Sync marar sa hannu idan ba a saita wani sirri na cikin gida ba (#13679). Duk sa hannun da yake akwai ana tabbatar da shi koyaushe — kuma ana ƙin karɓarsa koyaushe idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` ba — ba tare da la’akari da wannan tuta ba. A v3.9, tsohon saitin zai koma tilasta wannan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`      | `false`                                                   | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`      | Saita zuwa `true` don komawa ga halayyar mataki ɗaya ta v3.8.5 ta “shigo da komai” ba tare da tabbatarwar mai amfani ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                      | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | OAuth, Allon sarrafawa, aiki tare                | URL na jama'a don OAuth redirect_uri, hanyoyin haɗin Allon sarrafawa, da URL na jama'a da aka samar. Saita wannan zuwa tsayayyen URL na jama'a lokacin da kiran-baya na OAuth ko hanyoyin haɗin burauza da aka samar dole ne su yi amfani da daidaitaccen mai masaukin reverse-proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                     | _(babu komai)_                                            | Bangaren abokin hulɗa                            | Kwafin `CLOUD_URL` na ɓangaren abokin hulɗa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`      | Madadin tsohon tsari na `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PORT`                          | _(ba a saita ba — yana komawa zuwa `PORT`)_               | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`          | Madadin tashar jiragen ruwa ta ɓangaren abokin hulɗa don URL na nuni idan ba a san tushensu ba (SSR/gwaje); ana karanta shi kafin `PORT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | Mai warware asalin jama'a, URL na hotuna         | Asalin OmniRoute da burauza ke fuskanta mai fifiko mafi girma, wanda ake amfani da shi don samar da URL na jama'a da kuma tabbatar da asalin burauza wanda ba na dashboard ba. Saita wannan lokacin da OpenWebUI ko wani relay ke isa OmniRoute ta URL na ciki, amma burauzar mai amfani dole ne ya ɗauko kafofin watsa labarai da aka samar daga LAN, tunnel, ko asalin jama'a. Kada a haɗa `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`           | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Cikakken URL na manifest ɗin plugin na mai bayarwa da ake sanar da abokan cinikin sidecar. Idan ba a saita shi ba, OmniRoute yana samo `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` daga asalin buƙata ko HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`                 | `http`                                                    | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Yarjejeniyar da ake amfani da ita yayin samo URL na manifest ɗin plugin na mai bayarwa daga HOST/PORT ba tare da asalin buƙata ba. Saita zuwa `https` a bayan wakilin jama'a mai ƙare TLS idan ba a saita takamaiman `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`              | Yanayin amincewa na zaɓi don kanun asalin jama'a da aka tura. Ba a saita ba = kada a amince da `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` don yanke shawarar tsaro. `true` / `loopback` yana amincewa da host/proto da aka tura ne kawai daga wakilin loopback mai tambarin token. `private` / `lan` yana kuma amincewa da abokan wakili na LAN mai zaman kansa, sannan yana barin gadar `API_PORT` ta riƙe `X-Forwarded-For` na irin wannan abokin. A samarwa, fi son takamaiman `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | URL na callback na jama'a don ayyukan kie.ai marasa jira. | `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Wata hanyar rubuta `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`. | `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Asalin jama'a da ake amfani da shi wajen haɗa URL na callback marasa jira. Shi ne madadin mafi ƙarancin fifiko don callback na kie.ai; ana kuma amfani da shi a matsayin URL na jama'a na gama-gari don sauran relay.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`              | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`                     | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`      | Maƙurar amfani ta OpenCode Go ta hukuma, wadda ke amfani da tantancewar API key kuma shafin Usage ke amfani da ita. Sauya ta don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`           | `true`                                                    | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Ƙirƙiri kanun shaidar OpenCode CLI (User-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, UUIDs na request/session) a kan buƙatun upstream na opencode-go/zen waɗanda client bai aika ba, domin Cloudflare ya karɓe su yayin fita daga VPS (#6210/#5997). Yana kunne ta tsohuwa tun daga #10571; kashe shi da `false`/`0`/`no`/`off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                       | `opencode/1.18.31`                                        | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | Tsohuwar User-Agent da ake amfani da ita idan `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` yana kunne kuma ba a saita keɓantaccen `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` na kowane provider ba. Ana amfani da ita ne kawai ga opencode executors. A kan buƙatar da ba ta da key wadda upstream ke tacewa, ana maye gurbin ƙimar da aka saita wadda ba ta ɗauke da `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` da wannan tsohuwar ƙima maimakon a ƙi buƙatar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARD_ATTRIBUTION`             | `1` (kunne)                                               | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | Tura kanun danganta app na client (`x-session-id`, `x-title`, `http-referer`) zuwa upstream providers, domin buƙatar da ta fara a cikin harness da aka jera ta ci gaba da riƙe shaidarta ta cikin router (matatun publisher na OpenRouter suna dogara da waɗannan). `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` yana riƙe shaidar registry/operator kuma yana watsar da ƙimomin da client ya bayar. Kanun musamman da operator ya saita koyaushe suna rinjayar ƙimomin client.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                           | `desktop`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Ƙimar kanun `x-opencode-client` da aka ƙirƙira idan `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` yana kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                          | `global`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Ƙimar kanun `x-opencode-project` da aka ƙirƙira idan `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` yana kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`        | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Ingantaccen jerin providers waɗanda ake rarraba gazawar upstream ɗinsu bisa egress IP (429 guda ɗaya yana dakatar da duk wata haɗin sibling da ke amfani da IP ɗin). Idan ba a saita ba, ana amfani da tsohuwar ƙimar free-tier `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` yana kashe wannan cascade gaba ɗaya; jerin da waƙafi ya raba yana maye gurbin jerin. Upstream yana rarraba paid plans bisa account kuma za su iya kashe shi cikin aminci (#9611).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_QUOTA_ROTATION_PROVIDERS` | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Iyakokin juyawar hanyar fita idan an ƙi saboda ƙa'idar amfani. Idan ba a saita ba, za a ajiye wani memba na tafkin a gefe idan an samu 429 kawai ga rukunin da aka kasafta bisa IP da ke sama; `all` yana faɗaɗa wannan aiki zuwa kowane mai samarwa, ta yadda idan aka samu iyakar ƙimar buƙata ta hannun wani memba, zaɓi na gaba zai yi amfani da wata hanyar fita. Duk wata ƙima ban da `all` za a yi watsi da ita. Yana buƙatar `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED` (yana kunne ta tsohuwa). Ana yi wa ma'ajiyar mabuɗi da adireshin hanyar fita, ba da (mai samarwa, adireshi) ba, don haka faɗaɗa 429 yana dakatar da wannan memba ga kowane mai samarwa har sai wa'adin ya wuce (minti 5, yana ninkuwa zuwa 15). Ana karantawa ga kowace sakamakon amsa, don haka canji zai fara aiki ba tare da sake kunnawa ba. |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Saita zuwa `off` don dakatar da daidaita jikin buƙatun OpenCode marasa maɓalli (tutar watsa kai-tsaye da jerin kayan aiki). Har yanzu ana amfani da kanun buƙata. Ana karantawa ga kowace buƙata, don haka canji zai fara aiki nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`      | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Sunayen kayan aiki da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za a ayyana a buƙatar OpenCode marar maɓalli wadda ba ta ɗauke da ko ɗaya, idan har yanzu ba a lura da wani abu ga wannan samfurin ba. Idan babu komai, za a koma ga kayan aiki guda ɗaya na wucin gadi wanda aka umurci samfurin kada ya kira. Aƙalla shigarwa 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; ana yin watsi da shigarwar da ba su inganta ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_CREDENTIAL`             | `public`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Bayanan tantancewa da ake aikawa a hanyar matakin kyauta mai kariya (`https://opencode.ai/inference/v1`). Tun daga 2026-10-21 wannan hanyar tana mayar da 403 `Model access is disabled` ga ainihin maɓallin API, kuma tana amfani da ƙimar zahiri ta marar-suna a maimakonsa. `key` yana riƙe maɓallin API na mai gudanarwa, `none`/`off` ba ya aika kanun bayanan tantancewa, kuma duk wata ƙimar zahiri dabam ana aikawa a matsayin token na bearer. Ana karantawa ga kowace buƙata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`          | `https://ollama.com/settings`                             | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | URL na saitunan Ollama Cloud da ake amfani da shi don tattara bayanan ƙa'idar amfani. Sauya shi don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_BALANCE_URL`        | `https://ollama.com/api/balance`                          | `open-sse/services/ollamaCloudBalance.ts`        | Tashar upstream ta ma'aunin kuɗi ta Ollama Cloud da aka rubuta a takardu (`GET https://ollama.com/api/balance`, `Authorization: Bearer <API key>`). Ana karanta ta sau ɗaya lokacin loda module. Ana fara amfani da ita don ƙa'idar amfani ta Ollama Cloud idan haɗin yana da maɓallin API; mai tattara bayanai daga shafin saituna ta cookie yana nan a matsayin madadin. Sauya shi don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Cookie na Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` da ake amfani da shi don tattara bayanan ƙa'idar amfani daga shafin saituna. Mai muhimmancin tsaro ne; fi son filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                                         | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Madadin env var na cookie na Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session`. Mai muhimmancin tsaro ne; fi son filin Dashboard na kowane haɗi idan an saita asusu da yawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`             | _(unset)_                                                 | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Madadin env var na kuki `__Secure-session` na Ollama Cloud wanda ake amfani da shi kafin gajerun sunayen madadin. Bayani ne mai matuƙar muhimmanci; idan an saita asusu da yawa, an fi son amfani da filin Dashboard na kowace haɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`          | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`           | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint na iyakokin amfani da CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Sauya shi don relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Lokacin turawa a bayan reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa tsayayyen URL ɗinka na jama'a (misali, `https://omniroute.example.com`) idan OAuth callbacks ko hanyoyin haɗin jama'a da aka ƙirƙira dole su yi amfani da wannan hostname. Idan babu wannan, OAuth callbacks na iya gaza saboda redirect_uri ba zai dace ba, kuma hanyoyin haɗin jama'a da aka ƙirƙira na iya nuna asalin container na ciki.
>
> Bar `BASE_URL` a matsayin URL na loopback/container na ciki don ayyukan server-to-server. Kada a yi amfani da `Origin` na burauza ko hostname na jama'a don internal self-fetches masu ɗauke da bayanan tantancewa.
>
> Rubuce-rubucen dashboard da aka tantance ba sa buƙatar tsayayyen base URL na jama'a: dashboard yana aika unsafe requests masu same-origin tare da CSRF token da ke daure da session. Har yanzu OmniRoute yana tattara tabbatar da public-origin a wuri guda don haɗin gwiwar burauza da ba na dashboard ba: ana fara amincewa da takamaiman env vars na URL na jama'a; ana yin watsi da ɗanyen headers na `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` sai dai idan an kunna `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` kuma an yi wa peer na proxy na kai tsaye alamar token a matsayin amintacce. Kada a yi amfani da saitunan CORS don gyara requests na dashboard masu same-origin; CORS na abokan hulɗar burauza masu cross-origin ne kawai.

---

## 8. Proxy na Fita

Yi wa kiran masu samar da LLM na sama (upstream) hanya ta wata HTTP ko SOCKS5 proxy domin sarrafa fita zuwa waje, tsara hanyoyin yanki, ko ɓoye adreshin IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` tana da tsoffin ƙima `true`. Saita ita zuwa `false`, `0`, `no`, ko
`off` domin tilasta HTTP/1.1 a kan dispatchers na Undici a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`
da `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: fita kai tsaye, proxies na HTTP(S)/SOCKS, relays, da
sake gwadawarsu ta sababbin sockets. Wannan yana kiyaye tabbatar da takardar shaidar TLS, tantancewar
proxy, iyakokin haɗi, da saitunan keep-alive. Sake ƙaddamar da OmniRoute bayan
canza ta saboda ana adana dispatchers a cache kuma ana ƙirƙirar relay agents a lokacin ƙaddamarwa.
Wannan maɓallin ba ya sarrafa wreq TLS fingerprinting ko transports na musamman na kowane mai samarwa.

| Mai canji                                | Asali             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                           | Kunna wakilin SOCKS5 proxy don kiran upstream. A fice da shi da `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | Bangaren abokin ciniki                         | Sanin bangaren abokin ciniki game da samuwar SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`            | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasali da ke kunne ta asali (dubi [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); wani override na dashboard DB yana cin nasara). Proxy pools da juyawar kowane asusu suna daina sake amfani da memba wanda ya yi gazawa kwanan nan (wanda ya ƙi amsa binciken TCP, ko 429 da ta shiga ta hannunsa) na wani lokaci wanda ke ninkin biyu a kowane maimaitawa, har zuwa iyaka. `false` yana maida zaɓin yau da kullum.                                                                |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Jerin izini don manufofin sarrafa-selector na local-core bayan loopback (masauki ko adiresoshin IP da aka raba da waƙafi ko sarari, `*` yana buɗe LAN/private amma ba a taɓa buɗe metadata/link-local). Ana izina loopback a kan http/https ta asali. Idan an saita daraja a dashboard DB kuma ba fanko ba, tana cin nasara a kan wannan variable.                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Lokacin asali (ms) na keɓewar 429, wanda ke ninkin biyu a kowane maimaitawa. Ana karanta shi sau ɗaya lokacin ƙaddamarwa. An takaita shi zuwa `1000..3600000`; idan ya wuce iyaka ko ba a iya karanta shi ba, yana komawa na asali tare da gargaɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Iyaka (ms) ta keɓewar 429. Ana karanta shi sau ɗaya lokacin ƙaddamarwa. An takaita shi zuwa `1000..3600000`; idan ya wuce iyaka ko ba a iya karanta shi ba, yana komawa na asali tare da gargaɗi. Idan iyakar ta kasance ƙarƙashin asalin, tana komawa na asali. Rage iyakar yana gajarta manta jerin gazawa: ana share shigarwar `2 x max` bayan ƙarewa.                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Tutar fasali ta zaɓin-kunna (dubi [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); wani override na dashboard DB yana cin nasara). Ga masu samarwa waɗanda aka kwatance kuotar su bisa adireshin fita (egress), sanya memba na pool wanda ke raba adireshin fita da aka lura da shi na memba da aka ƙi kwanan nan a matsayi kaɗan ƙasa da mambobi masu lafiya. Tsari kawai, ba a taɓa keɓe su. Yana buƙatar `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, wanda ke samar da siginar ƙin da yake karantawa. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`          | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Tagar dawowa (ms) wadda ke takaita maimaita sanarwar webhook na `proxy.set_aside` ga kowane memba na pool, domin tarƙon ƙin yarda ya aika lamari ɗaya kawai. Ana karanta shi a kowane fitarwa. An takaita shi zuwa `1000..3600000`; idan ya wuce iyaka ko ba a iya karanta shi ba, yana komawa na asali a shiru.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Daidaitaccen Node.js                           | HTTP proxy don kiran upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Daidaitaccen Node.js                           | HTTPS proxy don kiran upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Daidaitaccen Node.js                           | Proxy gama-gari (yana goyon bayan `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Yana kafa manufar echo-IP da binciken fitowar proxy (egress) yake amfani da ita zuwa URL guda ɗaya. Idan ba a saita ba, binciken yana gwada `api64.ipify.org` sannan `api4.ipify.org` domin kada a yi rahoton cewa layukan IPv4-kawai sun mutu (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Daidaitaccen Node.js                           | Sunayen masaukin/IP da aka raba da waƙafi don wuce proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Mafi yawan sockets masu aiki a lokaci ɗaya ga kowanne dispatcher na proxy na HTTP/SOCKS da aka ajiye a cache. Dogayen rafukan SSE kamar Codex `/v1/responses` suna buƙatar fiye da haɗi ɗaya idan buƙatu da yawa suna amfani da proxy ɗaya na matakin asusu. Ana ƙuntata ƙimar da ta haura `256`.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Lokacin ƙarewa (timeout) na handshake (haɗi) na SOCKS5 a cikin ms. Ku ɗaga shi idan masauki ɗaya na ƙofar zama ya fuskanci matsananciyar yawan buƙatu a lokaci ɗaya (misali 100 buƙatu a lokaci ɗaya) — ainihin handshake na iya wuce 10s a ƙarƙashin cika-cikin pool ko da yake ana iya isa proxy, wanda in ba haka ba zai bayyana a matsayin ƙarya `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable`. An ƙuntata shi a `120000`.                                                            |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Idan `false` (na asali), ana **ƙin (fail-closed)** buƙata wadda proxy da aka sanya mata ta kasa warwarewa maimakon faɗowa zuwa haɗin kai tsaye — hakan yana hana zubowar ainihin IP. Saita `true` don maido da tsohon DIRECT fallback.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                           | Gurbata TLS fingerprint ta amfani da wreq-js (yana kwaikwayon Chrome 124). Yana mayar da martani ga toshewar JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Jerin izinin masu samarwa da aka raba da waƙafi don sabon jagorar TLS ta proxy (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). TLS na kai tsaye yana riƙe tsohon halinsa idan ba a saita ba; kawai waɗannan masu samarwa ne suke wucewa ta gadar fingerprint ta Chrome-124. An fitar da Groq (`api.groq.com`) ko da lokacin da ba a saita jerin izinin ba, saboda Chrome JA3 yana haifar da Cloudflare 1010 yayin da undici na asali yana isa API.                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Bari mahallin burauzar Playwright na Claude Turnstile ya yi watsi da kurakuran takaddun shaida na HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Yanayi

| Yanayi                                                   | Tsari                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 ta hanyar layin SSH**                           | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Proxy na HTTP na kamfani**                             | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Yaƙi da fingerprint**                                  | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — yana buƙatar `wreq-js` (an haɗa shi)                                                      |
| **Egress da ake sarrafawa / babu damar kai tsaye**       | Bar `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (na asali). Buƙatu suna gaza ƙwarai idan proxy ba ya samuwa maimakon zubo ta haɗin kai tsaye. |
| **Tsohon tsari / ci gaba — barin fallback na kai tsaye** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Yana maido da halin kafin ƙarfafawa: ana amfani da haɗin kai tsaye idan warware proxy ya gaza.    |

> **Lura (wucewar ingantaccen NVIDIA — #3226):** Ƙarshen inganta maɓallin API na NVIDIA
> yana tsayawa idan aka jagoranta shi ta hanyar fetch na duniya wanda aka dafa shi da proxy/TLS (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` yana da nufin wuce patch na proxy ga kiran ingantaccen ɗaya nan ta amfani da `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Wannan keɓancewa ne da aka rubuta kuma aka iyakance — **ba** ya shafar fitowar chat/amfani.
> Ana ɗaure ikon wucewa ta `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts`.

## 9. Haɗin Kayayyakin CLI

Yana sarrafa yadda OmniRoute ke gano da ƙaddamar da CLI sidecars (Claude Code, Codex, da sauransu).

| Mabambanta                        | Asali                                | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                               | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | `auto` = nemo a cikin PATH na tsarin; `manual` = yi amfani da hanyoyin da aka bayyana kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                    | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ƙarin shigarwar PATH don gano binary na CLI (an raba su da colon).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                    | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Maye gurbin kundin gida don karanta configs na CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Dole ne ya kasance cikakkiyar hanya (absolute) kuma a cikin gidan process — **ko**, a cikin container, hanya da aka yi bind-mount (wannan shine yadda `/host-home` ke aiki). Duk wani abu dabam yana komawa zuwa kundin gida.                                                                                         |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                               | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Bari OmniRoute ya rubuta fayilolin config na CLI (sabuntawar token, bayanan session). Saita zuwa `false` don kowane rubutu na config na CLI ya gaza tare da kuskuren "writes disabled" da aka bayyana a sarari.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                             | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Hanya ta musamman zuwa binary na Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Hanya ta musamman zuwa binary na Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Hanya ta musamman zuwa binary na Droid CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Hanya ta musamman zuwa binary na OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, sannan `cursor`             | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Hanya ta musamman zuwa binary na agent na Cursor. Idan babu ita, ganowa tana gwada `agent` da farko sannan ta koma zuwa `cursor`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_CLINE_BIN`                   | `cline`                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Hanya ta musamman zuwa binary na Cline CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_5DIVE_BIN`                   | `5dive`                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Hanya ta musamman zuwa binary na 5dive CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_5DIVE_STATE_DIR`             | `/var/lib/5dive`                     | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Kundin yanayin tsarin na 5dive (auth profiles da root ke mallaka); yana daidaitawa da tsohon asali na STATE_DIR na 5dive kanta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
