Saita takamaiman ID kamar `account:zai-start-plan` don ɗaure wannan bayanin martaba; wanda aka ɗaure ba ya taɓa komawa madadin. |

| `WHYCODES_HOME`                   | `~/.whycodes`                        | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/whycodesHome.ts` | Yana maye gurbin tushen WhyCodes da ke ɗauke da `config.toml`. Idan ba a saita shi ba, yana nemo `.whycodes` ƙarƙashin `HOME`, sannan `USERPROFILE`, sai kuma `os.homedir()`. Ana amfani da ƙimar maye gurbin yadda take kai tsaye; ba a faɗaɗa `~` da ke farkonta.                                                                                                                                 |

### Daidaitawar Profile na CLI ta Atomatik

Waɗannan tutocin fasali na zaɓi ne kuma tsoffin saitinsu a kashe suke. Haka kuma ana iya
canza su daga allon sarrafa CLI Code.

| Canji                                 | Tsoho   | Fayil na Tushe                                   | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin profile `~/.codex/*.config.toml` daga ƙasaɗa (catalog) mai rai a kai tsaye. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitin Codex mai aiki/tsoho, tantancewa, saitunan Codex-lb, ko zaɓin mai samarwa.     |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin profile na Claude Code `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` daga ƙasaɗa mai rai a kai tsaye. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitin Claude mai aiki/tsoho, tantancewa, ko zaɓin mai samarwa. |

### Misalin Docker

```bash
# Shigar da binary na mai masauki a cikin container kuma gaya wa OmniRoute inda suke:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` yana aiki ne kawai idan an yi bind-mount na hanyar daga mai masauki (host) — haɗa shi da mounts kamar `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (duba `host` profile a cikin `docker-compose.yml`). Ana watsi da hanya da ba ta cikin gidan mai amfani na container ba kuma ba ta bind mount ba, domin rubutawa a can za a jefar da ita sa'ad da aka sake ƙirƙirar container.

Hoton yana gudana a matsayin `USER node`, don haka `/root` da ba a mount ba **ba** ingantaccen override ba ne.

| Mai Canji                                | Tsoho         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------- | ----------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(kai-tsaye)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Ƙarfafa gano container a kunna (`1`/`true`) ko a kashe (`0`/`false`). Ana buƙatarsa ne kawai a runtimes da gano kai-tsaye bai isa ba.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Ƙyale rubutun config na kayan aikin CLI zuwa hanyar container da ba a mount ba duk da haka. Daidaitaccen abu a CLI shi ne `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                                   |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | ba a saita ba | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | Hoton Docker kawai: dashboard **Sabunta Yanzu** yana rubuta buƙatar JSON zuwa wannan fayil (dole ya kasance cikin `DATA_DIR`) domin mai sa ido na gefen host ya yi pull kuma ya sake ƙirƙirar container. Duba [Jagorar Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### Masu taimako na Binary na CLI (`omniroute`)

Waɗannan masu canji suna daidaita halayen binary na CLI na `omniroute` da kansa (ba gano sidecar da ke sama ba).

| Mai Canji                             | Tsoho                             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                         | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(tsarin)_                        | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Ƙarfafa harshen fitarwa na CLI. BCP-47 locale (misali `en`, `pt-BR`). Yana rinjayar masu canjin locale na tsarin (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` domin aika stdout/stderr na uwar garken zuwa terminal a yanayin kula (supervised mode). Daidai da tutar `--log` a `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token na tantance na'ura (machine-auth) da ake saka a matsayin header `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Ana ƙirƙirar shi kai-tsaye a aikin 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Tushen gida na Gemini na dindindin; gudanarwa ta sau ɗaya (one-shot) tana amfani da gida na wucin gadi da aka ware.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Lokacin jinkiri (grace period) a millisecond kafin a tilasta kashe child process na CLI da aka aika sigina.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa 1 tare da DATA_DIR da aka fayyace domin tsallake fayil ɗin muhalli na kundin bayanan tsohon mai amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Token na daidaitawa (sync) don `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` yana karɓar token na daidaitawa kawai, ba maɓallin API ba). Daidai da `--token`.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Ƙarewar lokacin HTTP (timeout) ga kowane yunƙuri (ms) don buƙatun CLI → uwar garken.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Mafi girman lokaci (ms) da CLI yake jira don health endpoint na uwar garken kafin ya buga gargaɗin ƙarewar lokaci. Yana da amfani ga cold starts masu jinkiri (misali Windows). Hakanan ana iya saita shi ta `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Saita zuwa `1` domin buga binciken retry/backoff zuwa stderr yayin umarnin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Kundin da aka keɓance don gano plugins na CLI (paketeji `omniroute-cmd-*`). Tsoho shi ne `~/.omniroute/plugins/` idan ba a saita ba. CLI kawai — bai taba kai wa mai duba plugins na gefen uwar garken ba, wanda `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` yake nuna (sashe na 2).                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Yana kashe binciken sabuntawa idan an saita kowace ƙima marar fanko: sanarwar CLI lokacin da tsari ya ƙare da banner na “akwai sabuntawa” a allon kulawa (`GET /api/system/version` sai ya tsallake binciken npm, GitHub da labarai). Sanarwar CLI tana nan a kashe a CI, yanayin shiru da fitarwa marar rubutu.                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(an samo)_                       | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | URL na liveness da `omniroute doctor` yake gwada. Zaɓin `livenessUrl` na shiri (programmatic) yana da fifiko; in ba haka ba ana amfani da wannan ƙimar kamar yadda yake. Idan ba a saita ba, CLI yana samun endpoint `/api/health/degradation` na dashboard na gida.                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Hanyar katalog na masu samarwa da umarnin CLI na masu samarwa suke amfani da ita. Zaɓin `catalogPath` na shiri yana da fifiko. Ana amfani da absolute paths kamar yadda suke; relative paths suna warwarewa daga tushen aikin. Idan ba a saita ba, CLI yana amfani da kundin tsayayyun ƙimomi na masu samarwa, tare da fayil ɗin gado `providers.ts` a matsayin madadin. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(tsohon mai samarwa)_            | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Ƙirar madadin duniya don gwaje-gwajen haɗi na masu samarwa na CLI. Fifiko shi ne ƙirar haɗi da aka saita, override na ƙirar musamman mai samarwa, wannan ƙima, sannan ƙirar gwaji da aka gina cikin mai samarwa.                                                                                                                                                         |

## 10. Haɗin Agent na Ciki & MCP

| Mai Canji                                       | Tsoho                                               | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | ganowa ta atomatik                                  | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL na musamman don kayan aikin MCP/A2A su isa OmniRoute. Yana ƙetare ganowa ta localhost ta atomatik.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Modulolin MCP/A2A                                           | Mabuɗin API don kiran kayan aikin MCP na ciki da ƙwarewar A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID na mabuɗi don danganta log na binciken MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Gadar OAuth CLI (Na Ciki)

| Mai canji           | Tsoho              | Fayil ɗin tushe                 | Bayani                                              |
| ------------------- | ------------------ | ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | ganewa ta atomatik | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL na uwar garken don gadar auth ta CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token na auth don gadar CLI.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`              | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | ID na mai amfani don zaman gadar CLI.               |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Laƙabin gado na `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.                 |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Laƙabin gado na `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.                  |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(ba a saita ba)_  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Laƙabin gado na `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.                |

## 11. Takardun Shaida na Mai Bayar da OAuth

### Sabunta token na Codex a yanayin karɓa kawai

`OMNIROUTE_TOKEN_REFRESH_PASSIVE_PROVIDERS` da aka saita zuwa `codex` yana sa wannan instance ya zama mai amfani da bayanan shaidar Codex da aka daidaita, ba mai mallakar juyawar token na sabuntawa ba. Ƙimar asali mai fanko ko wadda ba a saita ba tana riƙe halayyar sabuntawa ta al'ada. Ana cire fararen sarari na gefunan ƙimomin da aka raba da waƙafi, kuma ba a bambanta manyan da ƙananan haruffa; `cx` wani sunan `codex` ne. Masu bayarwa da ba a tallafa musu ba suna sa tantancewa a farawa ta gaza. Sauran masu bayarwa ba su canja ba.

A wuraren sabuntawa da suke akwai, instance yana ketare cache na karanta haɗi kuma ya karɓi token na shiga dabam wanda bai ƙare ba kawai. Token da upstream ya ƙi a lokacin buƙatar ba za su sake cancanta ba bayan an mayar da daidaitawar baya: bayanan shaidar buƙatar suna riƙe alamun SHA-256 da ba su wuce huɗu ba, waɗanda ake zubarwa tare da wannan buƙatar. Filayen token kaɗai ake karɓa, don haka metadata na mai bayarwa da ya shafi wannan buƙatar yana nan. Ba a adana bayanan shaidar da aka karɓa na dindindin. Idan babu abin maye, `refresh_disabled` ba yanayi na ƙarshe ba ne kuma ba a ƙidaya shi cikin circuit breaker na sabunta token. Ainihin amsoshin upstream 401/403 suna riƙe lissafin gazawa na al'ada, lokacin jira mai ƙaruwa ta exponential da kurakuran da ake mayar wa abokin ciniki; alamun kashe akaunti har yanzu suna haifar da kullewa na dindindin.

Sabuntawa da hannu ta dashboard/API tana mayar da `409` da code `refresh_disabled` da umarnin sabuntawa a instance mai aiki sannan a daidaita. Sabuntawa da ba a nema kai tsaye ba (chat, amfani, binciken lafiya da tantance shigo da bayanai) ba ta nuna wannan kuskuren; farfaɗowa daga 401 wajen tambayar amfani idan an buƙata tana sake gwadawa sau ɗaya da token da aka daidaita kuma aka karɓa. Shigo da bayanan tabbatarwa na Codex yana adana token da aka bayar ba tare da musayar token na sabuntawa ba; sabbin shiga ta OAuth ba su canja ba. Ba a ƙara daidaitawa kafin aikawa a wajen hanyar sabuntawa tun da wuri da take akwai ba.

A riƙe mai mallakar juyawar token guda ɗaya tak, kuma a shirya daidaitawar token a daban kafin token na shiga su ƙare (lokacin rigakafi na asali: minti biyar). Wannan zaɓin ba kullewar rarrabawa ba ne, ba kwafin rumbun bayanai ba ne, kuma ba rabon shigar SQLite ba ne. Tushe: `open-sse/services/tokenRefresh/passiveCodex.ts`.

Takardun shaida da aka gina a ciki don **ci gaban localhost**. Don tura ayyuka a nesa (remote deployments), yi rajistar naka a kofar masu haɓakawa ta kowane mai bayarwa.

Yi nuni zuwa `/v1` self-loop na OmniRoute ko kowace ƙarshen hanya (endpoint) mai jituwa da OpenAI (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Issue #2232. Idan URL ɗin shine `/v1` na OmniRoute kanta, ƙaramar buƙatar describe tana aika `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` kuma tana tantancewa da shaidar self-loop da aka warware (`sk_omniroute` sentinel a yanayin local, ko `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) domin deployments na `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` su yi aiki. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Vision Bridge guardrail | Mabuɗin API don URL ɗin da ke sama. Yana maye gurbin env vars na OpenAI / Google na kowace mai samarwa don kiran vision-bridge waɗanda ba na Anthropic ba ne. Samfuran Anthropic suna ci gaba da amfani da hanyar mabuɗin Anthropic na musamman. Issue #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Vision Bridge guardrail | Tsawon lokacin da za a adana sakamakon jigilar "babu ɗan takara da za a iya amfani da shi" a cache, a ms (tsoho `30000`). Ƙima mara inganci ko mara kyau tana komawa zuwa tsohon; `0` yana kashe negative cache ɗin. Tushe: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Je zuwa [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Ƙirƙiri OAuth 2.0 Client ID (nau'i: "Web application")
> 3. Ƙara URL na umarwar ka a matsayin Authorized redirect URI
> 4. Maye gurbin ƙimomin takardun shaida a cikin `.env`.

## 12. Sauya User-Agent na Masu Bayar da Sabis

Sauya kanun `User-Agent` da ake aikawa zuwa ga kowane mai bayar da sabis na sama. Ana warware wannan ta hanya mai canzawa a lokacin gudanarwa ta hanyar ajin tushe na mai aiwatarwa:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Tushe:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Mai Canji | Darajar Tsoho | Yaushe Ake Sabuntawa |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Lokacin da Anthropic ta fitar da sabon sigar CLI |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Saita zuwa `1`/`true` don aika sunayen kayan aikin harness na ɓangare na uku kalma-da-kalma zuwa Anthropic a dukkan hanyoyin da ke zuwa Anthropic (native OAuth da CLIProxyAPI). A tsoho, mai aiwatarwa yana ba da sunayen barkwanci a hanya tabbatacciya ga sunayen kayan aikin da ba na Claude Code ba (Claude Code canonical mapping inda akwai, in ba haka ba PascalCase) kuma yana mayar da su a kan amsar ta `_toolNameMap`, don haka ba a ƙin harnesses masu kayan aikin snake_case azaman abokan ciniki na ɓangare na uku da aka gano alamomin su. Domin gano matsaloli kawai. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Lokacin da OpenAI ta sabunta Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Sauya sigar abokin ciniki na Codex kai tsaye ba tare da cikakken jerin UA ba |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Sauya `x-grok-client-version` don inference, daidaita samfuri, da OAuth na lambar na'ura, tare da tsohon User-Agent na Grok CLI don inference da daidaita samfuri. Yana karɓar SemVer tare da zaɓin metadata na prerelease/build, har zuwa haruffa 128 bayan an cire sararin da ke kewaye. Ƙimomin da ba a saita ba, fanko, marasa inganci, ko masu ɗauke da karya layi suna amfani da pin da aka aika. Sauya cikakken User-Agent na inference da ke akwai yana ci gaba da fifiko. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Sauya sigar Claude Code da aka sanar kai tsaye ba tare da `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` ba. Anthropic na takaita wasu samfuri bisa wannan ƙima (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Sauya haɗin ƙarshe na haruffa 3 da OmniRoute ke haɗawa zuwa `cc_version=` a cikin toshe na billing na Claude. Ƙara shi tare da `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — kafkafa siga kawai yana sanar da ma'aurata `version.revision` da babu ainihin binary da ke fitar da ita. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Sauya sigar Copilot CLI da aka sanar kai tsaye ba tare da `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` ba |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Lokacin da GitHub Copilot Chat ya sabunta |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CLI_CL` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Yana maye gurbin jerin canje-canjen Piper na Antigravity CLI (`agy`) da aka bayar a matsayin `cl=` cikin User-Agent na CLI. Ba za a samo shi daga sigar ba, kuma ba a nuna shi cikin manifests na jama'a; ana cika shi ga kowace siga daga ainihin kamawa (1.4.4 → `1003595037`). Idan ba a saita shi ga sigar da ba a sani ba, ana barin `cl=` maimakon sanar da jerin canje-canjen wani build dabam. |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Lokacin da Kiro IDE ya sabunta |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Sauya `clientId` na zamantakewa na lambar na'ura ta Kiro (id na jama'a) |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Ta zaɓi: cikakken tantance CRC na saƙon kowace frame a kan kwararar abubuwan Kiro (gwada kwararar da suka lalace) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD ga kowace bashi na Kiro; yana mayar da bashin `meteringEvent` zuwa ainihin kuɗin kowace buƙata |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Kashe amsar Kiro da ba ta ba da rahoton auna bashi ba maimakon rubuta ta ba tare da lissafa kuɗi ba |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Lokacin da Qoder CLI ya sabunta |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Lokacin da Cursor ya sabunta |

> [!TIP]
> Kuna iya ƙara sauya User-Agent ga **kowane** mai bayar da sabis ta amfani da tsarin `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Mai aiwatarwa yana gina sunan canjin muhalli ta hanya mai canzawa.

## 13. Daidaituwar Sawun Yatsa na CLI

Idan aka kunna, OmniRoute yana sake tsara taken HTTP da filayen jikin JSON domin su dace da ainihin sa hannun kayan aikin CLI na hukuma. Wannan yana rage haɗarin yi wa asusu alama, tare da kiyaye IP na proxy ɗinka.

**Tushe:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Na Kowane Mai Bayarwa

| Sauyi                    | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                           |
| ------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cline          |

### Na Gabaɗaya

| Sauyi            | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                                             |
| ---------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`    | Kunna daidaituwar sawun yatsa ga **duk** masu bayarwa lokaci guda. |

### Sauya bayanan asalin Kimi Coding CLI

| Sauyi                   | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`                | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya sigar Kimi CLI da ake aikawa yayin kiran OAuth/API.                             |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(tsohon da aka ɗauka)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya ID na na'urar Kimi da aka ɗauka, wanda ake amfani da shi a taken abokin ciniki. |

> [!NOTE]
> Wannan fasalin yana aiki tare da sauye-sauyen User-Agent (§12). Tsarin sawun yatsa yana kula da jerin taken da jerin filayen jiki, yayin da sauye-sauyen User-Agent ke kula da takamaiman zaren UA. Ana iya kunna kowannensu da kansa.
