## 14. Masu Bayar da Maɓallin API

Maɓallan API na masu bayarwa waɗanda ke amfani da tantancewa kai tsaye. **Saitin da aka fi so:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Saita su ta hanyar sauye-sauyen muhalli wata hanya ce ta daban don girkawar Docker ko tsarin da ba shi da dubawar allo.

Tsarin da ake ganewa: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Sauyi              | Mai Bayarwa                                         |
| ------------------ | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                            |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                          |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + madadin Reader)           |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (laƙabi na `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)               |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + madadin taɗi |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (laƙabi na `GEMINI_API_KEY`)                 |

> [!NOTE]
> An cire shigarwar `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` na dindindin don Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, da Qianfan a v3.8.0 saboda runtime ba ya ƙara karanta su — waɗannan masu bayarwa suna dogara ne kawai da Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB mai ɓoyewa. Duba sashen _Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Amfani_ a ƙasan wannan takarda don hanyar ƙaura.

> [!TIP]
> Maɓallan da aka saita ta Dashboard ana adana su da ɓoyewa a SQLite kuma suna da fifiko kan sauye-sauyen muhalli.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings na `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, da `jina-search` ba sa cajin maɓallin muhallin cluster idan akwai haɗin dashboard na `jina-ai` (ko `jina-reader` na bai ɗaya) — `getProviderCredentials` yana cike na farko. Ana amfani da `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Katin Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) ba ya taɓa ba da `/v1/embeddings` ko `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (laƙabi `google/gemini-embedding-2`) yana fara amfani da haɗin `gemini` na dashboard. Ana amfani da `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta madadin muhalli da `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Zirga-zirgar multimodal ta asali tana amfani da `x-goog-api-key` zuwa `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — abubuwan OpenAI `input` guda N suna zama vectors guda N.

---

## 15. Saitunan Lokacin Ƙarewa

Dukkan ƙimomi suna cikin **milisecond**. Ana warware su a wuri guda a cikin `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Matsayin Lokutan Ƙarewa

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (global override)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (upstream provider calls, default: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (independent, default: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (independent, default: 0 = off, suggested: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (independent, default: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa; ba a saita ba: 1260000, budgets da aka ayyana suna ɗaga shi; an saita: iyaka ta dindindin; 0 yana kashe shi)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (caps adaptive readiness extensions, default: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (derived, default: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 0 = disabled)
```

| Maɓalli                                         | Tsoho                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(unset)_                                                         | Gajeriyar hanya ta duniya — tana rinjayar tsoffin ƙimomin `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Jimillar lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar HTTP ga kiran mai samarwa na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin shiru tsakanin ɗanyen bytes na sama kafin a soke aikin. Samfuran tunani mai zurfi ba safai suke yin tsayawa sama da 90s ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Matsakaicin jimillar tsawon rayuwar rafin SSE mai aiki; ba ya sake farawa idan an karɓi bytes daga sama, kuma ya kasance mai zaman kansa daga `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. Idan ba a saita shi ba, tsohon ƙimar ita ce mafi girman `timeoutMs` na kowane samfuri a cikin rajista (1200000, Codex) tare da ƙarin 60000, kuma target da ya ayyana kasafin lokaci mafi tsawo (connection `timeoutMs`, sannan na kowane samfuri, sannan na matakin mai samarwa, misali GLM's 3000000) zai samu wannan kasafin tare da ƙarin 60000. Ƙima mai kyau ita ce iyaka mai tsauri ga kowane target da kasafin da aka ayyana ba za su iya ƙara ba. Saita zuwa `0` don kashe shi ga kowane target.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(disabled)_                                                      | Ko OmniRoute zai iya fitar da layukan sharhi na SSE `:` (misali bugun zuciyar `: keepalive` da ƙarin bayanan metadata na `x-omniroute-*`). An kashe shi ta tsohuwa (#10524) saboda abokan cinikin da suka dace da OpenAI kuma suke da tsauraran ƙa'idoji suna yin JSON.parse ga kowace layin SSE kuma su faɗi saboda sharhin `:`; bugun zuciyar `data:` bai shafe shi ba. Saita zuwa `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` don sake kunna shi. Ana amfani da shi ta `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SSE_COALESCE_MS`                               | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Zaɓi ne: haɗa abubuwan `content_block_delta` na Claude masu zuwa jere (mai fihirisar toshe da nau’in delta iri ɗaya) da ake aika wa abokan cinikin Claude, tare da aika su aƙalla kowane N ms. Ba ya rasa bayanai; ba a taɓa haɗa `signature_delta` da abubuwan kan iyaka ba. Yana aiki ga kowane nau’in delta sai dai idan an saita mabambantan canji na kowane nau’i a ƙasa. `open-sse/utils/sseDeltaCoalesce.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SSE_COALESCE_THINKING_MS`                      | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Sake tsara ƙima ta kowane nau’i ta `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `thinking_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SSE_COALESCE_TEXT_MS`                          | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Sake tsara ƙima ta kowane nau’i ta `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `text_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SSE_COALESCE_INPUT_JSON_MS`                    | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Sake tsara ƙima ta kowane nau’i ta `SSE_COALESCE_MS` don `input_json_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SSE_FRAME_COALESCE_MS`                         | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Zaɓi ne: tattara firam ɗin SSE da aka tura cikin N ms zuwa rubutu guda da ake aika wa abokin ciniki. Ba a taɓa jinkirta firam na farko da firam na ƙarshe (`[DONE]`, `message_stop`, `error`, `response.completed`/`failed`/`incomplete`) ba; idan an kunna tattarawa, ITL yana auna gibin tsakanin tarin firam. `open-sse/utils/sseOutputSink.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SSE_STALL_WATCHDOG`                            | `timer`                                                           | Aiwatar da mai sa ido kan tsayawar baiti don amsoshin da ake watsawa. `timer` yana sake kunna mai ƙidayar lokaci ɗaya ga kowane gutsuren bayanai daga sama (yana aiki daidai lokacin da kasafin tsayawa ya cika); `interval` yana riƙe da bincike guda ɗaya ga kowane rafi tare da tazarar `clamp(budget/4, 1s, 5s)`, don haka tsayawa zai faru cikin kasafin tsayawa + tazarar bincike. `open-sse/utils/streamStallWatchdog.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Lokacin karɓar taron SSE na farko wanda ba ping ba. Yana gādon `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran shirye-shiryen farkon abin daidaitawa don manyan buƙatun yawo masu amfani da kayan aiki da yawa ko buƙatun da ke buƙatar zurfin tunani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Maɓallin kashewa nan take na ƙa’idar binciken `/goal`. Saita zuwa `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe ganowa gaba ɗaya — ba a taɓa ƙara lokutan jiran shirye-shirye da dawo da yawo bisa ga jikin buƙata/kanun buƙata ba, hakan yana rage yiwuwar abokin ciniki ya ƙara lokacin jira fiye da kima.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran shirye-shiryen farkon abu don tafiyar da agent ɗin `/goal` da aka gano ko buƙatun da aka tilasta musu ta `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kunna dawo da yawo da wuri ta atomatik don tafiyar da agent ɗin `/goal` da aka gano. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe zaɓin `/goal` na musamman. Wannan na iya ƘARA dawo da yawo ne kawai a kan tsoffin saitunan mai gudanarwa — ba ya taɓa rinjayar zaɓin kashewa na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB da aka fayyace kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Cire abubuwan SSE marasa bin ƙa’ida na `codex.*` (misali, `codex.rate_limits`) waɗanda ke jawo `responses.stream()` na OpenAI SDK ya dawo da 502. Tsoho KUNNA (#11014). Saita `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` don tura su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(unset)_                                                         | Zaɓin jigilar Codex ta app-server. Ƙarshen WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) na wani `codex app-server` na gefe na gida. Idan an saita shi tare da alama, ana tura buƙatun Codex ta JSON-RPC zuwa app-server na gefe maimakon HTTP Responses API. Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ga kowace haɗi ta `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. Ana amfani da shi a `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(unset)_                                                         | Alamar izini/bearer da aka saka kai tsaye kuma ake gabatarwa ga app-server. Sauyawa ga kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(unset)_                                                         | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin da ke riƙe da token na damar app-server (daga `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). Ana amfani da shi lokacin da ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` ba. Sauyawa ta kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Kundin aiki da juyawar app-server ke gudana a ciki. Sauyawa ta kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(unset)_                                                         | Manufar amincewa da ake miƙawa ga juyawar app-server (misali, `never`, `on-request`). Sauyawa ta kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(unset)_                                                         | Manufar sandbox da ake miƙawa ga juyawar app-server (misali, `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Idan ba a saita ta ba, executor yana amfani da `workspace-write` ta tsohuwa (mai ƙarin tsaro; a baya `danger-full-access`). Sauyawa ta kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | A amince ta atomatik da tambayoyin amincewa na app-server da kansa (aiwatar da umarni/fayil/izini a kan host). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — ana ƙin tambayoyin ta atomatik; kiran kayan aikin harness bai shafi wannan ba (suna bi ta hanyar `item/tool/call` ta daban). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`yes`. Sauyawa ta kowane haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar kanun amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Matsakaicin lokacin jira kanun amsa a kan buƙatun streaming (ms). `0` yana kashe wannan iyaka; `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` na mai bayarwa da aka ayyana yana da fifiko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran fara amsa (ms) ga kowace ƙoƙarin kai tsaye ba tare da proxy ba. Idan lokacin ya ƙare, za a sake gwadawa sau ɗaya ta sabuwar socket; saita `0` don kashe iyakar kuma a riƙe halayen da suka gabata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (5 min)                                                  | Ƙaramin lokacin fara amsa (ms) ga ƙoƙarin kai tsaye ba tare da proxy ba wanda host ɗin da ake nufi yake na private/LAN/loopback (misali `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), domin kada cold start na Ollama na asali ya katse saboda tsohuwar 30s da ke sama. Yana aiki a matsayin iyaka ta ƙasa: lokacin jiran da ake amfani da shi a zahiri shi ne mafi girma tsakanin wannan ƙima da `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ko ƙimar da ba ta inganta ba yana cire iyakar ƙasar ta local. Ba ya shafar remote hosts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_VOLUME_ADAPTIVE`      | `1`                                                               | Tada taga amsa kai tsaye-fara ta hanyar buƙatun biyan kuɗi: jikin sama da 512 KB sami × 2, sama da 2 MB × 3, wanda aka rufe a 180 s kuma ba zai taɓa saukar da bene mafi girma ba (mai aiki ya tsallake ko bene na local/LAN a sama). Manyan mahallin suna cika daidai gwargwado zuwa girman shigarwar ba tare da wani dalili na zaɓin dalilin rufin don maɓalli a kai ba, don haka taga lebur ta yanke lafiyayyen jinkiri-cike sama a daidai ƙasa. Saita `0`/`false`/`off` don daidaita ɗabi'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (10 min)                                                 | Iyakar sama (ms) ga ƙoƙarin RETRY ta sabuwar socket bayan lokacin fara amsar ƙoƙarin pooled ya ƙare a sama (#13703). Yana aiki ne kawai idan mai kira ya riga ya haɗa siginar wa’adinsa (cascade na resolved connection/model/provider/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`); wannan siginar ita ce ainihin iyaka kuma tana aiki da farko a hanyar da aka nufa, don haka wannan madadin kariya ce mai faɗi maimakon madaidaicin cap — ba tare da shi ba, retry ɗin zai sake amfani da taga gajere iri ɗaya ta `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` kamar ƙoƙarin pooled, kuma ya ba da 504 ga reasoning models masu lafiya amma masu jinkirin TTFB. Ba a taɓa barin sa ya kasance ƙasa da iyakar madaidaiciya da ke sama ba; idan mai kira bai samar da siginar deadline ba kwata-kwata, retry ɗin yana riƙe da iyakar madaidaiciya ba tare da canji ba. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar cikakken jikin amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar kafa haɗin TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Lokacin ƙarewar rashin aiki na socket mai keep-alive.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin ƙarewa na wakilin TLS fingerprint (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Yana iyakance lokacin zuwa byte na farko a jikin jigilar TLS-fingerprint ta wreq-js musamman; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` shi kaɗai ba zai iya gano jikin da ya tsaya ba, domin yana warwarewa da zarar kanun bayanai sun iso (#12656). Lokacin ƙarewa yana soke mai karanta wreq sannan ya koma ga mai rarraba kai tsaye/wakili; `0` yana kashe wannan watchdog.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kaɗai, kuma sai idan tutar fasalin `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` tana kunne (kashe ta ne a tsohuwa): yana iyakance jiran byte na farko na jikin amsar Responses da ake watsawa bayan kanun bayanai (#13484). Rafin Responses yana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka yin shiru bayan wannan tazara alama ce ta tsayawa: ana sanyaya asusun kuma buƙatar tana komawa zuwa asusu na gaba sau ɗaya; tsayawa ta biyu tana gaza nan take. `0` yana kashe kariyar ko da tutar tana kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= kashe)                                                     | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kaɗai: yana iyakance jiran kanun amsar upstream a kiran Responses da ake watsawa yayin da har yanzu akwai wani asusu — buƙatar da aka jera tana daina jira ta wuce gaba maimakon ta riƙe cikakken lokacin jiran kanun bayanai. Kiran Responses da ake watsawa ne kaɗai abin ya shafa (rafin Responses yana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka yin shiru a can jerin gwiwa ne, ba samarwa ba); kammalawar hira da kiran da ba a watsa ba ba su taɓu ba, kuma wannan tazara tana rage iyakar fara fetch mai tasiri ne kawai, ba ta ƙara shi ba. Ƙimar da aka ba da shawara yayin kunnawa: `30000`. `0` yana kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kaɗai: adadin lokutan da iyakar jiran kanun bayanai da ke sama za ta iya matsar da buƙata zuwa asusu na gaba a kowace buƙata. Asusu na ƙarshe da ya rage koyaushe yana riƙe cikakken lokacin jiran kanun bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kaɗai: ajiye buƙatar tare da heartbeat bayan 429 na wucin gadi da yawa (ko sabon alamar matsin tafkin), sannan a sake kunna wani zagaye guda mai iyaka wanda ya ƙunshi har zuwa asusu 3 a jere maimakon a rarraba ta ga dukkan rundunar (#13924). A kashe yake a tsohuwa: kowane 429 yana komawa zuwa asusu na gaba kamar yadda yake a da.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`           | `false`                                                           | Ga mai aiwatar da OpenCode kaɗai: idan duk asusun da lokacin sanyayarsu ya ƙare a zagayen sake kunnawa na ajiye-da-ci-gaba an ware su saboda ƙwaƙwalwar ƙin amincewar wakili, a yi musu hidima duk da haka maimakon a sake kunna komai (#14851, ci gaba ne na #14750). A kashe yake a tsohuwa: zagayen sake kunnawa ba ya ƙunsar komai a wannan yanayin, kamar yadda yake kafin #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Don tattaunawar yawo kawai: idan jikin upstream na farko ya tsaya kafin ya samar da wani lamari mai amfani, a yi ƙoƙari na biyu mai iyaka ta hanyar routing iri ɗaya, da kasafin shirye-shirye iri ɗaya, ba tare da hukunta asusu ba. An kashe shi ta tsohuwa: jikin farko da ya tsaya yana sa buƙatar ta gaza ba tare da sake gwadawa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | Don mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: bayan 429 daga mai bayarwa da aka rarraba bisa guga na egress a kan asusun da ba shi da proxy a cikin mahallin pool na yanzu, a nemi wani memba daga pool ɗin haɗi don ƙoƙari na gaba maimakon sake gwada adireshin egress ɗin nan. Yana tsara fifiko, ba ya warewa: pool da ya ƙare yana ci gaba da halayen yanzu. An kashe shi ta tsohuwa: kowane 429 yana juya zuwa asusu na gaba kamar yadda yake a da.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(unset)_                                                         | Don mai aiwatar da OpenCode kawai: canja hanyar alamar matsin pool da ake karantawa kafin a dakatar (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, tsoho `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Sabuwar alama tana dakatarwa ba tare da sake ƙirga ba; idan babu ko ta tsufa, ana komawa ga ma'aunin burst.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar tsallen proxy don buƙatun bridge na `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Nuna mai aiwatar da debo yanar gizo na Firecrawl zuwa wani misali da ake karɓar bakuncinsa da kansa (mabuɗin API na zaɓi ne idan ba a cikin girgije ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewa na kowane buƙata don mai aiwatar da debo yanar gizo na Firecrawl.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TAVILY_BASE_URL`                               | _(unset)_                                                         | Tushen URL na mai debo ƙimar `/usage` na Tavily (Iyakar Mai Bayarwa); ana watsi da shi idan yana nuna zuwa `api.tavily.com`. Idan ba a saita shi ba, ana komawa zuwa tushen URL na haɗin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `300000`                                                          | Jimillar lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar uwar garken gada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                                                           | Lokacin aika kanun amsa ta gada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Lokacin ƙarewar zaman rashin aiki na keep-alive na gada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Lokacin ƙarewar raw socket (0 = an kashe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin rangwame bayan SIGTERM/SIGINT kafin tilasta fita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Ƙimar madadin da `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` ke amfani da ita lokacin da ba a saita `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Lokacin ƙarewa (ms) na saitunan `validationRead` da `modelsProbe` a cikin `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Ƙara shi don wuraren ƙarshe masu jinkiri (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) domin hana sauyawa akai-akai tsakanin aiki/kuskure a dashboard. Yana komawa zuwa 8000ms idan ƙimar ba ta inganta ba (<1000) ko ba lamba ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar fetch na musamman ga relay a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Relay da ya makale dole ne ya gaza kafin lokacin ƙarewar client/agent (~30s), domin masu kira su ga gazawar da ta shafi relay maimakon gazawar upstream ta gaba ɗaya. An iyakance shi zuwa `29000` domin koyaushe ya fara aiki da farko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Jinkirin sake gwadawa na bai ɗaya don hanyoyin direct/relay/proxy masu sake gwadawa sau ɗaya a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar wreq-js na asali (`claudeTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin sassauci na ƙayyadadden iyakar lokaci na JS, wanda ake ƙarawa sama da lokacin ƙarewar na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar wreq-js na asali (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin sassauci na ƙayyadadden iyakar lokaci na JS, wanda ake ƙarawa sama da lokacin ƙarewar na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (kashe)                                                       | Yana ƙara "Kana da ginanniyar binciken yanar gizo. Amsa tambayoyi kai tsaye ta amfani da sakamakon bincike." zuwa saƙon tsarin mai kira (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — Perplexity yana yin bincike ko ta yaya, kuma jimlar tana shiga amsoshi a matsayin bayanin-meta ga abokan cinikin coding. Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` domin maidowa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Ƙayyadadden lokacin jiran buƙatar wreq-js na asali (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin sassaucin wa'adin ƙarshe na JS gaba ɗaya, wanda ake ƙarawa a kan lokacin jiran na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Ƙayyadadden lokacin jiran buƙatar wreq-js na asali (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` yana ƙara shi zuwa `180000` ga kowace buƙata don samarwa masu tsawo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin sassaucin wa'adin ƙarshe na JS gaba ɗaya, wanda ake ƙarawa a kan lokacin jiran na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Rukunin burauzar Playwright da aka raba don tattaunawar kukin yanar gizo da burauza ke tallafawa (`browserPool.ts`); saita shi zuwa `off` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Hanyar zuwa binary na `obscura` da ake amfani da shi a matsayin injin farko ta rukunin burauzar da mai aiwatar da Cloudflare Playground (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); ana gano shi ta atomatik daga tsarin PATH idan ba a saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(unset)_                                                         | Nuna wani Obscura da ke aiki tuni (`http://host:port`) maimakon ƙaddamar da wani; tsarin ba shi ne ke kula da wannan aikin ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Takamaiman tashar jiragen ruwa don `obscura serve` da aka ƙaddamar; ana zaɓar tashar da ba ta aiki ta atomatik idan ba a saita ta ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Zaɓi buƙatar tattaunawar kuki ta yanar gizo ta bi hanyar da ke amfani da burauza (`browserBackedChat.ts`); saita zuwa `1` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | Tushen URL na mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (Connect-RPC na kimi.ai na ƙasa da ƙasa) (`kimi-web.ts`); a canza shi kawai don wuraren madubi/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Cikakken ƙarshen hanyar tattaunawa na mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(auto-detected)_                                                 | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin aiwatarwa na Chrome/Edge na tsarin don shiga Adobe Firefly ta burauza mai hulɗa (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); yana rinjayar gano ta atomatik bisa tsarin aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _.build/ standalone output_                                       | Canjin lokacin gini don kundin fitowar standalone da matakin tattara bayan gini ke amfani da shi (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); kayan aikin gini ne, ba na lokacin aiki ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

Ƙoƙarin kowace manufa ta combo yana gadon lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar upstream da aka warware (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ko
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan shi ne ke samar da tsohon ƙimar fetch). Saita `targetTimeoutMs` a cikin combo,
tsoffin ƙimomin combo, ko override na mai samarwa ne kawai idan ana son komawar combo ta yi sauri; ana takaita ƙimomin da suka fi
lokacin ƙarewar upstream na yanzu zuwa wannan lokacin ƙarewar upstream.

`comboTimeoutMs` kasafin lokaci ne na daban na agogon bango ga dukan combo, wanda ya shafi dukkan manufofin failover.
Bar shi ba a saita ba ko saita zuwa `0` don ci gaba da juyawa marar iyaka (har yanzu ana amfani da
tsayawar ratayewa ta tilas mai mintuna 10 da aka rubuta kai tsaye a cikin
`comboPredicates.ts`). Ƙima mai kyau tana maye gurbin wannan
kariyar ga combo. Ka tabbatar `comboTimeoutMs` ya fi `targetTimeoutMs` tsawo domin failover
ya ci gaba da samun lokaci bayan manufa ta farko ta yi jinkiri.

### Ƙofofin Mai Katse Da'ira

Daidaita mai katse da'ira a matakin mai samarwa. Tsoffin ƙimomin sun yi daidai da ƙimomin da aka faɗaɗa da ake amfani da su tun v3.6 don haɗe-haɗe 500+.

| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Lokacin sake saitawa (ms) na breaker na mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samar da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Lokacin sake saitawa (ms) na breaker na mai samar da API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts`                 | Lokacin dakatarwa na kowane maɓalli (ms) da jujjuyawar maɓallan API masu yawa ke amfani da shi lokacin da 429 daga sabis na sama bai ƙunshi `Retry-After` ba (#14573). Ƙimomin da ba su da kyau ko waɗanda ba lambobi ba za su koma ga tsoffin ƙima.                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Ƙididdiga marasa inganci ko marasa ƙididdiga sun koma ga tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Tazarar hana sauyawa ba da gaira ba (ms) da ke jure gajerun lokutan dakatarwa na wucin gadi kafin a cire pin ɗin ajiyar mahallin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Yanayi

| Yanayi                                            | Tsarawa                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Samar da lambar da ke ɗaukar lokaci mai tsawo** | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (minti 15)                             |
| **Ƙayyade jimillar tsawon rayuwar rafin**         | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (minti 21)                      |
| **Gaggauta kasa ga API na samarwa**               | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                                |
| **Samfuran tunani mai tsawo**                     | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (minti 5 tsakanin gutsure-gutsure) |
