An kashe ta tsaṅwa: ana binciken kowane proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_PASSIVE_WINDOW_MS`                         | `300000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/passiveVerdict.ts`                                                    | Taga a cikin ms na zirga-zirgar samarwa da tsallake passive ke dubawa (an taƙaita `60000..599999`). Ta fi gajera da tsaƙanin share minti 10 domin sabuwar gazawa ta yi mulki maimakon ta yallece.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                     | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                           | Ƙyale OAuth da hanyoyin tabbatar da mai samarwa su wuce proxy da aka tsayar kuma su haɗu kai tsaye lokacin da farkon binciken isa ga proxy ya gaza. Fifiko na gaske shi ne Feature Flags DB override > env var > tsaṅwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_WAIT_MS`                          | `30000` (30s)               | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts`                                                           | Kasafin **jiran-layi** na tila: tsawon lokacin da buƙata na iya jira don samun wuri na mai samarwa kuma ta zauna a cikin layi kafin a ƙi ta, ba tare da ta kai wa mai hawan sama ba. Ana share ƙidayar lokacin da zarar aikin ya fara gudana — ana ƙuntata gudanarwa daban ta hanyar `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`. Muhalli yana saita tila kawai: `resilienceSettings.requestQueue.maxWaitMs` da aka adana, sannan `rateLimitOverrides.maxWaitMs` na kowane haɗi, kowannensu yana fi ta fifiko.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (10 min)           | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                    | Mafi girman tsawon lokacin da buƙata da aka shigar na iya zama a gudanarwa kafin ajiyar iyakar yawan buƙatunta ta ƙare — an raba shi da kasafin jiran-layi domin fara fetch a hankali a kan ƙofofi marasa-ci-gaba baya ƙarewa ta lokaci (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (an kashe)              | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                    | Iyakar shigar da layi: ƙi da 429 `queue_full` da zarar wannan adadin buƙatu sun riga suna cikin layi. `0` = babu iyaka (tila).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_           | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                    | Tilasta kunna ko kashe hanyar kariyar iyakar yawan buƙatu mai kunna-kanta ba tare da la'akari da saitin Dashboard da aka adana ba (`resilienceSettings.requestQueue.autoEnableApiKeyProviders`). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on` don tilasta kunna, `false`/`0`/`off` don tilasta kashewa. Lokacin da yake aiki (tila), ana kare haɗin API-key da ke aiki ta atomatik da iyakar yawan buƙatu. Saita `rateLimitProtection: false` na kowane haɗi yana kashe kariya ta bayyane amma ba ya cire shi daga wannan hanyar kariya; saita wannan canjin muhalli zuwa `false` shine hanyar gaba ɗaya don gudanar da haɗin API-key ba tare da layin iyakar yawan buƙatu ba.                      |

A KASHE na tila (yana mamaye Connection Cooldown / Provider Circuit Breaker). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on` don kunna shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                      | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                             | Ƙaramin lokacin sanyaya (ms) kafin a sake gwada mai samarwa/haɗi da ya gaza. Ana ƙara shi ta hanyar exponential tare da gazawo masu jere. Ana amfani da shi kawai lokacin da `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED` yake kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (5 min)            | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                             | Mafi girman iyakar lokacin sanyaya (ms) kafin a sake gwada mai samarwa/haɗi da ya gaza ko ya zama dole. Ana amfani da shi kawai lokacin da `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED` yake kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (shuka) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (dabarar) | **Menene:** maido da ragunan hanyoyin hawan sama da aka yanke ba tare da nuna kome ba (free-claude-code port). Yana riƙe tagar SSE ta farko har zuwa `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms) domin a sake buɗe kuma sake gwada yanke _pre-commit_ — wanda ke faruwa kafin kowane byte ya kai wa abokin ciniki — ba tare da bayyana ba. **Yaushe ake kunna:** hanyoyin hawan sama marasa tabbas waɗanda ke yawan yanke 0-byte a farkon rafi; bar shi A KASHE idan ba za ka iya jure har 750 ms na ƙarin lokacin-samun-farkon-token a kowane rafi ba. Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`. Yana shuka saitin Resilience da aka adana; saitin Dashboard yana cin nasara da zarar an saita shi. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (seed) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (logic)    | **Me:** ci gaba a tsakiyar rafi (Fase 4.4) — bayan yanke _post-commit_ (bytes sun riga sun isa ga abokin ciniki), sake yin buƙata tare da ɓangaren rubutu azaman prefill na mataimaki kuma a dinka suffix ɗin da ya ɓace. Rafuna masu jituwa da OpenAI na sauƙaƙan rubutu kawai; ba ya taɓa kunna yayin da akwai kiran kayan aiki yana tafiya. **Yaushe za a kunna:** doguwar ƙirƙira da ke yanke a tsakiyar amsa kuma kana karɓar wutsiyar da aka dawo ta isa a fashewa ɗaya maimakon token-da-token. Mai zaman kanta daga `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (sabanin bayanan haɗari). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                                       |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`               | Mai sa ido na zaɓi kan fitarwa mai amfani ta rafi mai aiki. Yana gano rafuna waɗanda ke ci gaba da aika chunks amma suna kasancewa ƙasa da ƙimar fitarwar mataimaki da aka saita; heartbeats, abubuwan amfani, deltas maras komai, da matakan kayan aiki/hankali ba sa ɓoyewa a matsayin ci gaba. Daban ne daga timeout na idle da na wa'adi mai ƙarfi. Yana saita ƙimar tsoho ne kawai: ƙimar gaskiya/ƙarya da aka adana a `resilienceSettings` (Allon sarrafawa → Saituna → Juriya) tana da fifiko.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                     | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                 | Lokacin kariya kafin kimantar throughput, iyakance zuwa 0–600000 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                     | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                 | Tagar fitarwa mai amfani mai juyawa, iyakance zuwa 1000–600000 ms; ana buƙatar cikakken taga ɗaya kafin a fasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                         | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                 | Mafi ƙarancin ƙimar byte na fitarwar mataimaki UTF-8 (kimanin token mai kiyayewa), iyakance zuwa 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                         | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                 | Mafi ƙarancin samfurin fitarwa mai amfani wanda ba sifili ba da ake ɗauka ana iya aunawa, iyakance zuwa 1–1000000 bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                              | Tazarar jinkiri (ms) tsakanin binciken lafiya na token na masu samarwa a lokacin farawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                       | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                              | Mafi ƙarancin jitter bazuwar (ms) da ake ƙarawa bisa `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin binciken lafiya na token na masu samarwa, don hana fashewa kwatsam (Matsala #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                              | Mafi girman jitter bazuwar (ms) da ake ƙarawa bisa `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin binciken lafiya na token na masu samarwa, don hana fashewa kwatsam (Matsala #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                        | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                              | Girman rukunin bincike mai jituwa don juyin binciken lafiya na token a farawa; ƙima mafi girma suna bincika ƙarin haɗin gwiwa a daidaita, ƙima mafi ƙanƙanta suna rage nauyin fashewa (Matsala #7875, regression na #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                         | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                   | Adadin sake gwaji ta atomatik a kan amsoshin cooldown na matakin model kafin a mayar da kuskure ga abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                        | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                   | Mafi tsawon lokacin backoff (sakan) tsakanin sake gwadawa na cooldown. Ana takaita shi da wannan ƙima ko da menene `Retry-After` na upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`     | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                               | URL na proxy mai adana token na Headroom. Tsarin rayuwar dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) yana ƙaddamar da CLI na `headroom-ai` na gida a kan loopback ta tsoho; a yi override kawai don nufin wani proxy na Docker sidecar na waje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

### Ƙimomin daidaitawar dawo da rafi (stream-recovery)

Ana daidaita halayen riƙewar farfadowa (holdback) ta hanyar ƙimomi masu
ƙayyadaddu (hardcoded) a cikin
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), an nuna su anan don
nuni kawai — canza su yana buƙatar gyaran lambar (code), ba canjin env var ba:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — tsawon lokacin da ake riƙe tagar SSE
  ta farko domin a iya sake gwada yanke gajere (truncation) na farko kafin a tabbatar da kowane byte zuwa abokin ciniki.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — iyakar ƙarfi a kan tagar da aka riƙe; a tabbatar
  (flush + passthrough) da zarar an tara wannan adadin bytes, ko da mai ƙidayar lokaci bai cika ba.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — mafi yawan sake buɗewar rafi na upstream ba tare da bayyana ba
  yayin da riƙewar har yanzu ba a tabbatar da ita ba.

> **Ƙayyadadden ƙimar yawan buƙatu na tagar zamewa (sliding-window) na kowane mai samarwa (babu env var):** ƙayyadadden
> ƙimar yawan buƙatu na tagar zamewa na kowane mai samarwa wanda aka ɗauka daga FCC _fallback_ yana wanzu a cikin lambar
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, an haɗa shi ta
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) amma yana fitowa tare da **taswirar tsoho mara komai (empty default map)**
> kuma **bashi da env var na mai aiki** a yau — ana kunna shi kawai ta hanyar ƙugiyar gwaji (test hook) /
> gyaran lambar. An yi nufi a bar shi ba tare da jera shi a teburin da ke sama ba. Ƙayyadadden relay na kowane `(token, IP)`
> wanda _yana da_ hanyar saiti shi ne `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Hanyar sadarwa & Tashoshi).

| --------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`       | Ma'ajiyar lamba a tsarin `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`       | GitHub Personal Access Token mai izinin `issues:write`.                                                                                                                          |

## Ci gaba da kunna sabis a tsarin kyauta na Render

Render yana sa sabis ɗin yanar gizo na kyauta ya shiga yanayin barci bayan mintuna 15 ba tare da zirga-zirgar shigowa ba ([iyakokin sabis na kyauta](https://render.com/docs/free)). Mai ƙaddamarwa mai zaman kansa (`scripts/dev/run-standalone.mjs`, wanda hoton Docker yake amfani da shi) na iya aika buƙatu lokaci-lokaci zuwa URL ɗin jama’a na sabis. Wannan aikin yana kasancewa a kashe idan babu URL. Ana rubuta kunna shi a rajista, sannan ana ba da rahoton canje-canje kawai a sakamakon buƙatu. Buƙatun ba sa bin umarnin tura su zuwa wani adireshi.

| Ma’auni                      | Ƙimar asali               | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SELF_PING_ENABLED`          | A kunne idan an saita URL | `0`, `false`, `off` ko `no` yana kashe mai aika buƙatun. Kashe shi a tsare-tsaren biyan kuɗi idan ba a buƙatar buƙatu lokaci-lokaci.                                                                                                           |
| `SELF_PING_URL`              | `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL`     | URL na tushe na jama’a. Render yana samar da `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL` ga sabis na yanar gizo da shafukan da ba sa canzawa; duba [jagorar ma’aunan muhalli](https://render.com/docs/environment-variables).                                        |
| `SELF_PING_PATH`             | `/api/monitoring/health`  | Dole ya fara da `/` kuma ya warware zuwa adireshi mai asali ɗaya da URL na tushe. URL ko hanya marar inganci yana kashe mai aika buƙatun tare da gargaɗi.                                                                                      |
| `SELF_PING_INTERVAL_SECONDS` | `180`                     | Tazarar buƙatu a cikin daƙiƙu; buƙata ta farko tana gudana bayan daƙiƙu 60. Ƙimar da aka karanta dole ta kasance daga `30` zuwa `2147483`, tare da haɗa iyakokin biyu; ana amfani da `180` idan ƙimar ba ta da inganci ko ta fita daga zangon. |

Sabis da yake aiki a duk wata mai kwanaki 31 yana cin sa’o’in instance 744. Kwatanta wannan amfani da rabon kyauta na Render na yanzu, wanda ake rabawa a duk sararin aiki.

## Yanayin Ƙaddamarwa

Don jagorar SRE ta backend na relay (halayen ts/bifrost/auto, wurin sanya 9router da CLIProxyAPI, da dabarar fallback mai yawan zirga-zirga), duba [Dabarar Backend na Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Ƙaramar Ci gaban Gida

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Samarwa ta Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
API_KEY_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Keɓaɓɓen Hanyar Sadarwa / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS mai Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
API_KEY_SECRET=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<wanda-aka-ƙirƙira>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

## 24. Skills Sandbox (v3.8.0+)

Iyakoki da saitunan tsaro da ake amfani da su lokacin da tsarin Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) ke aiwatar da ayyukan atomatik da masu amfani suka ayyana a cikin muhalli mai keɓewa.

| Sauyi                             | Tsoho                                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Iyakar lokacin agogo ga kowace aiwatarwar lambar skill a cikin sandbox. Iyaka ce tabbatacciya; ana kashe duk abin da ya wuce ta.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(yana komawa ga `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Iyakar lokacin tsara aiwatar da skill a babban mataki. Saita shi sama da `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` don ba da damar jerin ayyuka masu matakai da yawa.                                                                                                                                |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da skill zai iya karantawa daga kowane fayil guda ɗaya a cikin sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin bytes da ake ɗauka daga kowace amsar HTTP guda ɗaya a cikin skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tabbatacciyar iyaka ga haruffan stdout/stderr da ake dawo da su daga kiran sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Saita `1`/`true` don ba da damar sadarwar fita daga cikin sandbox. Ta tsohuwa tana kasancewa **keɓantacciya** saboda tsaro.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(babu komai)_                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Jerin container images da aka ware da waƙafi waɗanda aka amince da su don aiwatarwa a sandbox. Idan babu komai, ana amfani da tsohon ginannen zaɓi kawai.                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(tsohon ginannen zaɓi)_                       | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Container image da ake amfani da shi lokacin ƙirƙirar sandbox mai dogaro da Docker. Sauya shi don ɗaure amfani da keɓaɓɓen base image mai ƙarfafa tsaro.                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                         | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Container runtime don keɓe skills: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` yana zaɓar runtime mafi dacewa da aka shigar bisa OS na host (Apple Container/OrbStack a macOS, WSL Container a Windows, Podman a Linux), sannan ya koma Docker idan babu. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Kunna `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` yana buɗe hanyar fitar bayanai daga kowace lambar skill. Haɗa shi da `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` da tsauraran manufofin `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy a turawar da ake rabawa.

---

## 25. Ƙayyadaddun Masu Samarwa, Tunnels, Ajiyayyen Bayanai da Sauran Lokutan Gudanarwa

Soke fallback na alaƙar zaman Redis (ana kunna shi ne kawai idan an zaɓa; tsohon tsarin SQLite bai canza ba):

Kowane ma’ajiyar alaƙar Redis yana karɓar aƙalla ayyuka 8 da ba su kammala ba; idan ya kai iyakar, kira yana amfani da SQLite nan take, kuma guraben suna ci gaba da kasancewa a cike har sai aikin Redis na asali ya daidaita, ko da bayan wa’adin mai kira na 100 ms ya wuce.

- R1. Duk wata amsar Redis da aka kawo cikin lokacin aikin zaman ita ce tushen gaskiya: canje-canjen da aka karɓa, CAS da aka ƙi/ɓace, maɓallan da suka ɓace, gogewar maɓallan da ba su wanzu, da kuma nasarori ko rashin nasarar cirewa daga ajiya suna share layin fallback na gida na wannan zaman kai tsaye, ba tare da `await` tsakanin amsa da sharewa ba. Sharewar tana da aminci (a mafi muni, za a sake zaɓe sau ɗaya yayin wata katsewar aiki ta gaba); layin da ya tsufa ba shi da aminci.
- R2. Aikin da ya ƙare wa’adinsa ko ya samu kuskure kuma ya rubuta fallback na gida yana adana ainihin abin da ya rubuta a matsayin bajiti da aka tsara. Idan umarnin Redis ɗinsa ya kammala daga baya, SQLite compare-and-delete na aiki tare yana share wannan layin ne kawai idan bajitin ya yi daidai ƙwarai, ba ta hanyar daidaita asusu ko haɗin kai ba. Sabon rubutun da aka yi yayin katsewar aiki yana nan daram.
- R3. Cirewa daga ajiya koyaushe yana cire pin na gida na asusun da ya gaza, har ma a lokacin katsewar aiki; sarrafa amsarsa daga nan yana bin R1/R2. Redis mai lafiya ba ya taɓa rubuta pin na gida: babu kwafin madubi ko kundin tsara jeri, kuma samfurori na iya bambanta a lokacin katsewar aiki.

Ƙarshen quota na masu samarwa, tunnels na cibiyar sadarwa (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), tafkin fitar da bayanai na 1Proxy, ajiyayyen bayanan rumbun adana bayanai, da ƙananan sauye-sauye na kowane fasali da aka ambata a layin executor ko cikin scripts.

| Canjin yanayi                                  | Tsoho                                                                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SESSION_AFFINITY_STORE`             | `sqlite`                                                                    | `src/lib/db/sessionAffinity/storeFactory.ts`                              | Manhajar baya ta daidaita zaman da asusu. `redis` tare da saita `REDIS_URL` ne kawai ke kunna rabawa; ƙimomin da ba a saita ba ko waɗanda ba a sani ba, ko rashin tsarin Redis, suna amfani da SQLite. Yana buƙatar `sessionAffinityTtlMs` mai ƙima mai kyau. Hanyar Redis tana adana bayanan asalin asusun amfani da aka yi hash tare da ID ɗin haɗi; hanyar SQLite ta tsoho tana riƙe da tsarin JSON mai filaye huɗu na gado. Tana daidaita asali a cikin tafkin gida da ya cancanta kafin ID. Tana amfani da `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX` na abokin ciniki da aka raba, ƙimomin JSON da TTL na `PX`; canje-canje suna kwatanta ƙimar da aka lura da ita ainihin. Amsoshin Redis masu lafiya suna soke madadin katsewar gida, kuma amsa da ta makara za ta iya share takamaiman layin madadin da aka jera shi daidai, wanda wannan aikin da lokaci ya ƙare ya rubuta. Ana sake tabbatar da fil da aka samo tun da wuri lokacin amfani. Kurakuran Redis, ƙarewar lokaci ko cunkoson karɓa suna komawa SQLite tare da gargaɗi masu iyakancewa (iyakar 100 ms a kowane kiran ajiya); rashin samun sakamako daga Redis cikin nasara ba ya karanta shadow. Ba ya raba dagewar haɗin combo, fil ɗin zagayen Codex na asali, cunkoson asusu, kullewa ko iyakokin kashe kuɗi. |

Saita zuwa `0` don tilasta martanin JSON marar streaming ta ƙofar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `BIFROST_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                                     | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Lokacin ƙarewa na kowace buƙata yayin tura ta zuwa ƙofar Bifrost (ms). Idan lokacin ya ƙare, hanyar tana mayar da hanyar relay ta TS ta amfani da taken `X-Bifrost-Fallback`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_BIFROST_KEY`                        | _(unset)_                                                                   | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Laƙabi ne na `BIFROST_API_KEY` (scripts da ke karanta env ta hanyar `OMNIROUTE_*` ke amfani da shi). `BIFROST_API_KEY` yana da fifiko idan an saita su duka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND`                      | `ts` / `auto`                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/routingBackend.ts`                 | Ƙarshen relay na `/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`: `ts \| bifrost \| auto`. `ts` = relay na TypeScript (shi ne tsoho idan ba a saita Bifrost ba); `auto` yana zaɓar Bifrost idan an saita `BIFROST_BASE_URL` kuma `BIFROST_ENABLED` ≠ `0`, tare da komawa ta atomatik zuwa TS idan sidecar ba ya samuwa; `bifrost` yana tilasta amfani da Bifrost (tsayayye, babu komawa). Tabbatar da sahihanci, iyakar ƙima, kariyar allura, da jerin izini koyaushe suna gudana da farko a cikin Next route. Amsoshi suna ɗauke da `X-Routing-Backend` / `X-Routing-Fallback` / `X-Routing-Fallback-Reason`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

| `OMNIROUTE_BIFROST_FAILURE_COOLDOWN_MS`        | `5000`                                                                      | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrostCooldown.ts`                | Lokacin dakatarwa (ms) bayan hop na Bifrost sidecar ya gaza a yanayin `auto`, kafin relay ya sake gwada sidecar; yana tura buƙatu kai tsaye zuwa hanyar TS yayin da lokacin dakatarwar ke aiki, sannan ya sake bincikawa. `0` yana kashe wannan fasalin. Dole ne a haɗa ta da `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`; sannan uwar garken mai zaman kanta za ta ƙare TLS a kan mai sauraro iri ɗaya (`wss://` yana aiki ba tare da canji ba). Idan ba a saita ba → HTTP na yau da kullum. Samar da ɗaya kawai daga cert/key, ko hanyar da ba za a iya karantawa ba, yana rubuta gargaɗi kuma yana ci gaba da amfani da HTTP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

Wannan kariya ce ta ƙari tare da rarrabawar kariyar hanya ta `isLocalOnlyPath()` (`LOCAL_ONLY_API_PREFIXES` a cikin `src/server/authz/routeGuard.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Kunna da zaɓi (a kashe ta tsohuwa): idan `true`, `400` na yau da kullum (buƙatar da ba ta dace ba) shi ma yana jawo juyar da asusu. Wannan ƙari ne kawai — ba ya taɓa hana halin injin da yake akwai, inda `400` mai ɗauke da rubutun iyakar ƙima ko adadin amfani ke ci gaba da juyawa ko da wane darajar wannan alama take da ita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Adadin kurakuran `400` a cikin `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` da ake buƙata kafin a juya asusun (ana duba wannan ne kawai idan `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Tazarar lokaci mai motsi (daƙiƙu) da ake ƙirga kurakuran `400` a cikinta zuwa ga `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Mai Tsara Claude Warmup

Warmup da cron ke tafiyarwa don haɗin Anthropic OAuth da aka zaɓa, ta yadda buɗe tagar iyakar amfani ta sa’o’i 5 zai faru ne ta hanyar buƙatar tsarawa mai sauƙi maimakon buƙatar farko ta gaske (#8848). Mai tsara yana kashe a tsohuwa sai idan `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` yana da ƙimar truthy **kuma** an yi alamar haɗin a cikin `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; jerin haɗi maras komai yana nufin ba za a yi wa komai warmup ba ko da an kunna canjin muhalli.

| Variable                       | Default                     | Source File                  | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(unset → off)_             | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Babbar maɓallin kunna mai tsara warmup. Yana karɓar `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ba tare da la’akari da manyan ko ƙananan haruffa ba, kuma bayan cire sarari). Duk wata ƙima dabam, ko rashin saita ta, yana barin mai tsara a kashe. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Bayanin cron mai filaye biyar don lokacin warmup, wanda ake kimantawa a `America/Los_Angeles` (yankin lokacin sake saita Anthropic) ba tare da la’akari da agogon na’ura mai ɗauke da sabar ba.                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Yawan haɗin da ake yi wa warmup a layi ɗaya a kowane lokaci. Ana takaita shi zuwa `1`-`10`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba tana komawa zuwa `3`.                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Samfurin da ake amfani da shi don buƙatar warmup. A sauya shi ne kawai idan samfurin na tsohuwa bai samuwa a tsarin ku ba; a zaɓi samfurin mafi arha da har yanzu ke buɗe tagar.                                                |

### Zaman VNC na Shiga Ta Mai Bincike da Laƙabin Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC da aka saka cikin akwati, wanda ake amfani da shi wajen karɓar bayanan shaidar shiga ta mai bincike cikin hulɗa (`/api/vnc-session`), tare da tsohon laƙabin `DATA_DIR`. Dukansu zaɓi ne — saitunan VNC na tsohuwa suna nuni zuwa hoton `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` da aka haɗa, kuma ana sauya su ne kawai don hoton akwati na musamman, tashoshi, ko daidaita tsarin rayuwar akwati.

| Variable                              | Default                                                                  | Source File                                 | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Alamar hoton Docker na kwandon shiga na Chromium+VNC. Gina `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ko nuna wannan zuwa hoton al'ada.                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Binari na runtime na kwantena da ake amfani da shi wajen ƙaddamar da kwandon VNC (misali, saita shi zuwa `podman`).                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar VNC/noVNC da aka fallasa a cikin kwandon.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin kwandon.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar kundin bayanan martabar Chromium a cikin kwandon.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin mai masaukin da ke riƙe da bayanan martabar shiga burauza da aka adana.                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (10 min)                                                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin ƙarewa na rashin aiki (ms) kafin a share zaman VNC da ba ya aiki.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (30 min)                                                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iyakar tsawon rayuwa (ms) na zaman VNC guda ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin adadin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana lokaci guda.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin ƙarewa (ms) na jiran burauzar da ke cikin kwandon ta kasance a shirye don CDP.                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin ƙarewa (ms) na tattara zaman/cookies da aka kama bayan an kammala shiga.                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin tutocin layin umarni da ake miƙawa ga Chromium da ke cikin kwandon.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓantacciyar hanyar sadarwar Docker da kwandon shiga na VNC ke shiga (#12571) maimakon gada ta tsohuwa, don haka kwantena ’yan’uwa ba za su iya isa tashar gadar CDP ɗinsa ba.                                              |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(unset)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Sunan laƙabi na gado** na `DATA_DIR`, ana duba shi ne kawai bayan `DATA_DIR` da `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` duk ba a saita su ba. Yana gano ma'ajiyar zaman zaren yanar gizo na Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Tsarin Gwaji & E2E

Ana amfani da su ta `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, da `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. Bar kowace
ƙima da ke ƙasa ba a saita ba a cikin tura samarwa (production).

| Mai Canji                              | Tsoho                               | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Yanayin bootstrap na E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) ga mai gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | yana komawa zuwa `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kalmar sirri ta admin da aka shigar a cikin muhallin Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe binciken lafiya na gida yayin gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe madaukar binciken lafiya na token OAuth yayin gwaje-gwaje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(ba a saita ba)_                   | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Masu samarwa da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka fitar daga zagayowar sabunta token na gaggawa (misali `codex,openai`). Madadin da aka yi niyya ga kashe binciken lafiya gaba ɗaya — masu samarwa na gajeren TTL suna ci gaba da sabuntawa yayin da masu samarwa na cascade ke kasancewa masu amsa kawai. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe hayaniyar binciken lafiya a cikin Playwright stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Tsallake ginin production na Next.js kafin Playwright ya fara (kyautata CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                                 | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Tsallake hook na cire OmniRoute (CI yana amfani da shi don riƙe `node_modules` cikakke).                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                            | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Lokacin jira (ms) don uwar garke ta sami lafiya kafin gudanar da gwaje-gwajen ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`      | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL da tsarin smoke na Electron yake sa ran cewa aikace-aikacen da aka kunna zai bauta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jimlar ƙarar lokaci (ms) kafin tsarin smoke ya daina.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tagar daidaitawa (ms) bayan shafin ya lodi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(atomatik)_                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Hanyar da aka bayyana zuwa ga executable na Electron da aka kunna.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                          | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jagorar bayanai don gudanar da smoke na Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita `1` domin kiyaye kundayen bayanan gwajin smoke bayan gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita `1` domin yawo log na Electron zuwa stdout yayin gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: sake ƙaddamarwa a kan wannan kundayen bayanan kuma tabbatar cewa ƙaddamarwa ta biyu tana zaɓar direban SQLite na gargajiya.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(binciken PATH)_                   | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Sake rubuta hanyar binary na Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

### Layin sarrafa fassarar takardun

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (umarnin `npm run i18n:run`).
Ba a saita dukkan masu canji shida ba tare da saita su ba — saita su a cikin `.env` kawai
a kan na'urorin da ya kamata su iya gudanar da mai fassara takardun.

| Mai canji                                  | Na asali                | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | URL na tushe mai jituwa da OpenAI don backend na fassarawa.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`            | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Alamar Bearer don backend na fassarawa (ba a taɓa rajista shi ba).                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`              | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | ID na samfuri, misali `gpt-4o-mini` ko `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_FALLBACK_MODELS`    | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Sarkar samfuran da aka raba da waƙafi na zaɓi wanda ake gwadawa bisa tsari lokacin da samfurin farko ya gaza (429 quota, 401/402, 5xx, timeout, network); ba a sake gwada buƙata marar kyau (400/413/422) a wani wuri ba.                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`         | `60000`                 | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ƙaren lokaci na kowace buƙata a millisecond.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT`   | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Kulawar tunani na zaɓi, ana aika shi yadda yake zuwa backend idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`        | `4` (LLM), `8` (Google) | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ayyukan fassarar fayil/yanki a layi ɗaya. `--concurrency` yana maye gurbin saitin mahalli; ana sarrafa yawan buƙatun Google masu gudana a lokaci guda daban a ƙasa.                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_ENGINE`             | `llm`                   | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | `llm` yana amfani da backend na LLM da aka saita. `google` yana zaɓar Google a matsayin injin farko kuma baya buƙatar sauye-sauyen backend na LLM.                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_FALLBACK`    | `1`                     | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ana kunna shi ta tsohuwa tare da injin LLM: idan fassarar sashe ta gaza bayan sake gwajin tabbatarwa ko kuskuren samfur, ana gwada Google a matsayin madadin kuma ana tabbatar da fitowarsa. Saita `0` don kashe shi.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_API_KEY`     | _(ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Maɓallin Cloud Translation v2 na zaɓi. Idan ba a saita shi ba, buƙatu suna amfani da endpoint na yanar gizo marar maɓalli.                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_CONCURRENCY` | `6`                     | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Cikakkiyar lamba da ta fi sifili da ke iyakance buƙatun Google masu gudana a lokaci guda, wadda yankuna masu amfani da iyakar da aka saita iri ɗaya a cikin tsari guda suke rabawa. Ana ƙin ƙimomin da ba su inganta ba kafin kowace buƙata. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`                 | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Iyakar lokacin kowace buƙata a cikin milisekan, a matsayin cikakkiyar lamba daga `1` zuwa `2147483647`. Ana ƙin ƙimomin da ba su inganta ba kafin kowace buƙata.                                                                             |
| `UI_KIT_READ_TOKEN`                        | _(Ba a saita ba)_       | `scripts/check/check-ui-kit-upstream.mjs` | Alamar karantawa kawai don mai zaman kansa na sama UI kit repo, wanda `npm run check:ui-kit-upstream` ke amfani da shi da aikin yau da kullun "UI Kit upstream watch". A cikin gida cak ɗin yana amfani da bayanan git ɗin ku.               |
